            guard_notes.append(f"{ticker}:{n_excluded}")
        depth = sum(r for _, r in kept)
        dw = sum(pr * r for pr, r in kept) / depth if depth else None
        prices = [pr for pr, _ in kept]
        median = statistics.median(prices) if len(prices) >= 2 else None
        dev_med = ((dw - median) / median * 100.0) if (median and dw) else None
        # F3: exit_score_v1 rides this row (+0 writes). Funding/OI come
        # from the latest fresh perp snapshot (<=15 min); OI change is
        # vs 24h ago. Missing inputs -> partial score, never zero-filled.
        _p = perp_now.get(ticker)
        _funding = _p.get("funding") if _p else None
        _oi_chg = None
        if _p and _p.get("oi") is not None:
            _oi_old = _oi_24h_ago(ticker, cycle_dt)
            if _oi_old:
                _oi_chg = (_p["oi"] - _oi_old) / _oi_old * 100.0
        _sc = exit_score_v1(dev_med, depth, median7d.get(ticker),
                            red_status, red_close_h, _funding, _oi_chg)
        _exit = _sc["score_100"]
        _exit_ver = _sc["method_version"] if _exit is not None else None
        stmts.append((
            "INSERT INTO deviations (ticker_id, ts, depth_weighted_price,"
            " dev_vs_close_pct, dev_vs_median_pct, qualifying_depth_usd,"
            " pools_used, pools_excluded, redemption_status, por_age_hours,"
            " weekend_risk_score, exit_score, exit_score_version,"
            " method_version) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            [ticker, cycle_ts, dw, None, dev_med, depth, len(kept),
             n_excluded, None, None, None, _exit, _exit_ver,
             "v1-median-only"],
        ))

    # Hourly slot: redemption & PoR (both automated since Wave 3 / PoR v1).
    # state was already loaded above (F3 inputs); do NOT reload here.
    gap_bits = []
    if extra_gap_notes:
        gap_bits.extend([g for g in extra_gap_notes if g])
    if chain_errors:
        # Non-Solana only here (a Solana error returned early above):
        # that chain's pools stay missing this cycle; the Solana cycle
        # itself still succeeds. Isolation, like the perp cycle.
        gap_bits.append("dexscreener chain batch failed: " + "; ".join(
            f"{c}: {e}" for c, e in sorted(chain_errors.items())))
    if guard_notes:
        gap_bits.append(f"price-guard excluded pool(s): {', '.join(guard_notes)}")
    if fallback_used:
        gap_bits.append("dexscreener batch incomplete; per-token fallback used")
    if fallback_errors:
        gap_bits.append(f"per-token fallback errors: {fallback_errors} token(s)")
    if missing_now:
        gap_bits.append(f"{len(missing_now)} tracked pools missing from response")
    now_iso = cycle_dt.isoformat()
    _hourly_ran = False
    if dry_run:
        log(f"CYCLE {cycle_ts} DRY-RUN hourly slot skipped "
            f"(use --dry-run-hourly for redemption/PoR)")
    elif _hourly_due(cycle_dt, state):
        _hourly_ran = True
        # Wave 3: real hourly redemption observation (schedule + on-chain).
        # A failure here must never break the deviation cycle, so the
        # hourly write lives in its own pipeline call inside
        # run_hourly_redemption; only its gap fragment lands here.
        hourly_failed = False
        try:
            _frag = run_hourly_redemption(cycle_dt, state)
            if _frag:
                gap_bits.append(_frag)
                # Only a FAILURE fragment holds the clock back. An
                # informational fragment (on-chain PAUSE observed,
                # partial coverage honestly recorded) is a successful
                # observation — the clock advances.
                if "failed" in _frag:
                    hourly_failed = True
        except Exception as _e:
            gap_bits.append(f"hourly redemption failed: {_e}")
            hourly_failed = True
        # PoR v1 (ACC'd): same isolation pattern as redemption.
        try:
            _frag = run_hourly_por(cycle_dt, state)
            if _frag:
                gap_bits.append(_frag)
                if "failed" in _frag:
                    hourly_failed = True
        except Exception as _e:
            gap_bits.append(f"hourly PoR failed: {_e}")
            hourly_failed = True
        # F4 (penambahan 2026-11-12): liquidation guard rides the same
        # hourly slot. It NEVER holds the shared clock back — its
        # fragments deliberately avoid the word 'failed' (see
        # run_hourly_liquidation), so hourly_failed is untouched here.
        try:
            _frag = run_hourly_liquidation(cycle_dt, state)
            if _frag:
                gap_bits.append(_frag)
        except Exception as _e:
            gap_bits.append(f"hourly liquidation unavailable: {_e}")
        # S8 (fix ronde 8): the shared hourly clock advances ONLY when
        # BOTH hourly jobs succeeded. The old code advanced it
        # unconditionally, so a failed hour silently waited out the
        # next 55 min; a failed attempt instead backs off 15 min
        # (see _hourly_due) and is retried.
        if hourly_failed:
            state["last_hourly_failed_at"] = now_iso
        else:
            state["last_hourly_check"] = now_iso
            state.pop("last_hourly_failed_at", None)
    # F4 follow-up (DESAIN §3.2): while any ticker is warning/critical,
    # re-observe every 5 min until the condition clears. Bounded: only
    # while the alert set is non-empty, only on 5-min marks, and never
    # in the same cycle the hourly slot already observed (no double row).
    if (state.get("liquidation_alert_tickers") and not _hourly_ran
            and not dry_run and cycle_dt.minute % 5 == 0):
        try:
            _frag = run_hourly_liquidation(cycle_dt, state)
            if _frag:
                gap_bits.append(_frag)
        except Exception as _e:
            gap_bits.append(f"hourly liquidation follow-up unavailable: {_e}")
    # F2 (penambahan 2026-11-12): Monday Gap prediction (Sun >=22:00 UTC)
    # + outcome fill (Mon). No-op on other days; never breaks the cycle.
    # Skipped in dry-run (it writes monday_gap rows).
    if not dry_run:
        try:
            _frag = run_weekly_monday_gap(cycle_dt, state)
            if _frag:
                gap_bits.append(_frag)
        except Exception as _e:
            gap_bits.append(f"weekly monday gap unavailable: {_e}")
    # Equity-close slot: once per NYSE day after 16:30 ET (median-only mode).
    et = cycle_dt.astimezone(ET)
    if is_nyse_day(et) and (et.hour, et.minute) >= (16, 30):
        if state.get("last_equity_note_date") != et.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"):
            gap_bits.append(
                "equity-close: no licensed equity source configured "
                "(median-only mode; dev_vs_close_pct NULL)")
            state["last_equity_note_date"] = et.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    if not dry_run:
        save_state(state)

    # GG-16 / M1 (fix ronde 8): a cycle that wrote ZERO snapshots and
    # ZERO deviations is a FAILED cycle, never a success heartbeat. The
    # old code wrote cycles_ok=1 unconditionally here, so an HTTP 200
    # with no pairs refreshed the global freshness verdict forever
    # (healthSummary / the bot's 30-min gate look only at cycle_ts
    # and cycles_ok — nothing downstream reads gap_note).
    if n_snap == 0 and not per_ticker:
        gap_bits.append("empty response: no snapshots or deviations written")
        if dry_run:
            print(f"DRY-RUN CYCLE {cycle_ts} FAIL empty response: "
                  f"no snapshots or deviations written")
            return False
        try:
            pipeline([(
                "INSERT INTO poller_health (cycle_ts, cycles_expected, cycles_ok,"
                " failed_sources, gap_note) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
                [cycle_ts, 1, 0, "dexscreener",
                 "; ".join(gap_bits) if gap_bits else None],
            )])
        except Exception as e:
            log(f"CYCLE {cycle_ts} FAIL empty response + heartbeat write "
                f"failed: {e}")
        log(f"CYCLE {cycle_ts} FAIL empty response: no snapshots or "
            f"deviations written")
        return False

    if dry_run:
        # No Turso writes: print what WOULD have been written instead
        # of the final pipeline (snapshots + deviations + health row).
        print(f"DRY-RUN CYCLE {cycle_ts} OK tight={tight} "
              f"snapshots_would_write={n_snap} "
              f"deviations_would_write={len(per_ticker)} "
              f"missing={len(missing_now)} fallback={fallback_used} "
              f"dex_requests={dex_requests + fallback_requests} "
              f"chain_errors={chain_errors or '-'} "
              f"guard={';'.join(guard_notes) if guard_notes else '-'}")
        return True
    stmts.append((
        "INSERT INTO poller_health (cycle_ts, cycles_expected, cycles_ok,"
        " failed_sources, gap_note) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
        [cycle_ts, 1, 1, None, "; ".join(gap_bits) if gap_bits else None],
    ))
    try:
        pipeline(stmts)
    except Exception as e:
        # S10 (fix ronde 8): a failing final write still attempts a
        # FAILURE heartbeat, so the cycle is visible as failed instead
        # of vanishing with no heartbeat row at all.
        log(f"CYCLE {cycle_ts} FAIL final pipeline: {e}")
        try:
            pipeline([(
                "INSERT INTO poller_health (cycle_ts, cycles_expected, cycles_ok,"
                " failed_sources, gap_note) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
                [cycle_ts, 1, 0, "turso",
                 f"final pipeline failed: {e}"
                 + ("; " + "; ".join(gap_bits) if gap_bits else "")],
            )])
        except Exception as e2:
            log(f"CYCLE {cycle_ts} FAIL failure heartbeat also failed: {e2}")
        return False
    log(f"CYCLE {cycle_ts} OK tight={tight} snapshots={n_snap} "
        f"deviations={len(per_ticker)} missing={len(missing_now)} "
        f"fallback={fallback_used} dex_req={dex_requests + fallback_requests} "
        f"chain_errors={chain_errors or '-'} "
        f"guard={';'.join(guard_notes) if guard_notes else '-'}")
    return True


def main():
    if "--dry-run-multichain" in sys.argv:
        # Multichain 2026-11-12: one live DexScreener read per NEW chain
        # (Base, BSC, Robinhood) over the embedded seed set + the same
        # LIQ_MIN / price-guard / depth-gate math as run_cycle.
        # 0 Turso writes, 0 state writes. No lock needed (nothing shared
        # is written).
        return run_multichain_dry_run()
    if "--dry-run" in sys.argv:
        # Plain dry run: one DB-driven cycle (run_cycle dry_run=True) —
        # live DexScreener reads + full computation, 0 Turso writes, no
        # lock, no perp cycle (use --dry-run-perp for that), no hourly
        # jobs (use --dry-run-hourly). Multichain tickers appear here
        # only AFTER seed --live has written their pools to Turso; until
        # then use --dry-run-multichain for the seed set.
        now = datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
        ok = run_cycle(now, dry_run=True)
        if ok:
            print("DRY-RUN done: 0 Turso writes (snapshots/deviations/"
                  "health NOT written). Multichain tickers appear here "
                  "only after seed --live; see --dry-run-multichain.")
        return 0 if ok else 1
    if "--dry-run-hourly" in sys.argv:
        # Wave 3 verification: compute one hourly redemption observation
        # (live public-RPC reads) and print it WITHOUT writing anything.
        now = datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
        run_hourly_redemption(now, {}, dry_run=True)
        run_hourly_por(now, {}, dry_run=True)
        return 0
    if "--dry-run-perp" in sys.argv:
        # Penambahan 2026-11-12 (F1): one live Hyperliquid read
        # (allMids + metaAndAssetCtxs dex xyz) and print the rows that
        # WOULD be written to perp_snapshots. No Turso writes.
        now = datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
        run_cycle_perp(now, {}, dry_run=True)
        return 0
    if "--dry-run-liquidation" in sys.argv:
        # Penambahan 2026-11-12 (F4): one live mark+oracle read and
        # print the rows that WOULD be written to liquidation_watch.
        # No Turso writes.
        now = datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
        run_hourly_liquidation(now, {}, dry_run=True)
        return 0
    if "--dry-run-monday-gap" in sys.argv:
        # Penambahan 2026-11-12 (F2): run the weekly job for the CURRENT
        # moment and print what it WOULD write. Prediction only fires
        # Sunday >=22:00 UTC, outcome fill only Monday; other days this
        # prints nothing and exits 0. No Turso writes.
        now = datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
        frag = run_weekly_monday_gap(now, {}, dry_run=True)
        if frag:
            print(f"DRY-RUN monday_gap: {frag}")
        return 0
    if "--test-windows" in sys.argv:
        cases = [
            # (UTC iso, expected tight?) — Oct 24 2026 is Saturday.
            ("2026-10-24T11:00:00+00:00", False),  # Sat
            ("2026-10-26T13:00:00+00:00", True),   # Mon 09:00 ET (open-30)
            ("2026-10-26T13:29:00+00:00", True),   # Mon 09:29 ET
            ("2026-10-26T14:00:00+00:00", True),   # Mon 10:00 ET (open+30)
            ("2026-10-26T14:01:00+00:00", False),  # Mon 10:01 ET
            ("2026-10-26T19:30:00+00:00", True),   # Mon 15:30 ET (close-30)
            ("2026-10-26T20:30:00+00:00", True),   # Mon 16:30 ET (close+30)
            ("2026-10-26T20:31:00+00:00", False),  # Mon 16:31 ET
            ("2026-10-26T12:59:00+00:00", False),  # Mon 08:59 ET
            ("2026-12-25T14:00:00+00:00", False),  # Christmas (NYSE holiday)
            ("2026-11-01T14:00:00+00:00", False),  # Sunday
        ]
        ok = True
        for iso, exp in cases:
            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(iso)
            got = in_tight_window(dt)
            mark = "OK " if got == exp else "FAIL"
            if got != exp:
                ok = False
            print(f"{mark} {iso} ET={dt.astimezone(ET).strftime('%a %H:%M')} "
                  f"tight={got} expected={exp}")
        print("WINDOWS TEST:", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
        sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)

    lock_f = open(LOCK_PATH, "w")
    try:
        fcntl.flock(lock_f, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    except OSError:
        log("LOCKED another poller instance is running; exit")
        return 0
    now = datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
    if in_tight_window(now):
        # Loop every minute until the window ends (max 75 min safety).
        deadline = now + timedelta(minutes=75)
        while datetime.now(UTC) < deadline:
            cur = datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
            if not in_tight_window(cur):
                break
            # S10 (fix ronde 8): an uncaught cycle failure used to kill
            # the whole tight-window loop with no failure heartbeat.
            # A failed minute is logged and skipped; the loop continues
            # to the next minute (run_cycle itself writes a failure
            # heartbeat for the failures it catches).
            # F1: perp runs BEFORE the DexScreener cycle so a Hyperliquid
            # failure lands in this cycle's gap_note (never its own
            # health row — see run_cycle_perp). Never raises.
            perp_frag = _maybe_run_perp(cur)
            try:
                run_cycle(cur, extra_gap_notes=[perp_frag] if perp_frag else None)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"TIGHT CYCLE {cur.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')} "
                    f"FAILED (uncaught, continuing): {e}")
            nxt = (datetime.now(UTC).replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
                   + timedelta(minutes=1))
            time.sleep(max(0, (nxt - datetime.now(UTC)).total_seconds()) + 1)
        log("TIGHT LOOP done")
    else:
        # F1: perp runs BEFORE the DexScreener cycle (see tight loop).
        perp_frag = _maybe_run_perp(now)
        try:
            run_cycle(now, extra_gap_notes=[perp_frag] if perp_frag else None)
        except Exception as e:
            log(f"CYCLE FAILED (uncaught): {e}")
            return 1
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== KODE: schema.sql =====
-- Radar Tokenized Stocks — schema Tahap 1 (desain §2.1).
-- Semua waktu UTC (ISO 8601). Diterapkan via Hrana /v2/pipeline.
-- Catatan: snapshots_hourly / snapshots_daily dibuat oleh job agregasi
-- mingguan (retensi §2.2), bukan bagian skema awal ini.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS universe (
  ticker_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  underlying TEXT NOT NULL,
  issuer TEXT NOT NULL,
  legal_type TEXT,
  tier INTEGER NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  token_addresses TEXT,
  notes TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pools (
  pool_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  chain TEXT NOT NULL,
  dex TEXT NOT NULL,
  pair_address TEXT NOT NULL,
  quote_token TEXT,
  is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  min_liquidity_usd REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 100000
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snapshots (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  pool_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES pools(pool_id),
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  price_usd REAL,
  reserve_usd REAL,
  reserve_token REAL,
  reserve_quote REAL,
  volume_24h_usd REAL,
  source TEXT NOT NULL,
  raw_ref TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_snapshots_pool_ts ON snapshots(pool_id, ts);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_snapshots_ts ON snapshots(ts);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS equity_refs (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  underlying TEXT NOT NULL,
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  ref_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  price_usd REAL,
  source TEXT,
  corporate_action_note TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deviations (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  depth_weighted_price REAL,
  dev_vs_close_pct REAL,
  dev_vs_median_pct REAL,
  qualifying_depth_usd REAL,
  pools_used INTEGER,
  pools_excluded INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, -- written by the poller price-guard since 2026-10-24; declared here in fix ronde 9 (P1-4) after live PRAGMA verification 2026-11-09 (cid 12, INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 — the live DB already had it via an undocumented ALTER; see migrations/2026-11-09-pools-excluded.sql)
  redemption_status TEXT,
  por_age_hours REAL,
  weekend_risk_score REAL,
  -- F3 (penambahan 2026-11-12, DESAIN §2.3/§3.3): exit_score_v1, written
  -- by the poller with the deviations row (+0 writes). NULL when no
  -- component could be computed. Live ALTER via
  -- migrations/2026-11-12-penambahan.sql (check PRAGMA first).
  exit_score REAL,
  exit_score_version TEXT,
  method_version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'v1-median-only'
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_deviations_ticker_ts ON deviations(ticker_id, ts);

-- F1 (penambahan 2026-11-12, DESAIN §3.1): Hyperliquid XYZ perp snapshots,
-- one row per ticker per 5-min cycle. Only the 10 tickers with a live XYZ
-- perp (SPY/QQQ/GLD: no rows, never fabricated). funding/oi NULL when
-- the API does not publish them.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS perp_snapshots (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  perp_px REAL,
  funding REAL,
  oi REAL,
  dex TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'xyz'
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_perp_ticker_ts ON perp_snapshots(ticker_id, ts);

-- F4 (penambahan 2026-11-12, DESAIN §3.2): Liquidation Guard, one row per
-- ticker per hour (extra 5-min rows only while warning/critical).
-- liq_px_estimate ALWAYS NULL until a public liquidation-level source is
-- verified — never fabricated. No oracle_lag_s column: the public info
-- endpoints publish no oracle timestamp, so lag is unmeasurable.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS liquidation_watch (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  oracle_px REAL,
  mark_px REAL,
  liq_px_estimate REAL,
  distance_pct REAL,
  source TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_liqwatch_ticker_ts ON liquidation_watch(ticker_id, ts);

-- F2 (penambahan 2026-11-12, DESAIN §3.4): Monday Gap Predictor, ONE row
-- per ticker per week (week_start = Saturday). outcome stays 'pending'
-- until Monday's actual gap is computed — never guessed.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS monday_gap (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  week_start TEXT NOT NULL,
  gap_pred_pct REAL NOT NULL,
  confidence REAL NOT NULL,
  method_version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'monday_gap_v1',
  outcome_gap_pct REAL,
  outcome TEXT,
  outcome_ts TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_monday_gap_ticker_week ON monday_gap(ticker_id, week_start);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS redemption_windows (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  issuer TEXT NOT NULL,
  asset TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  window_note TEXT,
  window_note_en TEXT, -- EN twin of window_note (added 2026-10-25; live ALTER, backfilled row id=1)
  observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  source TEXT,
  next_open_at TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS por_feeds (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  issuer TEXT NOT NULL,
  feed_address TEXT,
  last_update_ts TEXT,
  age_hours REAL,
  reserves_vs_supply_note TEXT,
  checked_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- PoR v2 (ACC'd): per-token snapshots from the same hourly API responses
-- that feed por_feeds. Numbers stored as TEXT exactly as the API
-- publishes them (no float rounding); holdings kept for audit only.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS por_token_snapshots (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  shares_held TEXT,
  circulating_supply TEXT,
  holdings_json TEXT,
  checked_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- NOTE (Wave 3, 2026-10-25): alert_log.ticker_id deliberately has NO
-- REFERENCES universe(ticker_id). The log legitimately records aggregate
-- keys such as 'ALL' for batched alerts, which are not universe rows;
-- the FK made every batch record fail (first live batch, 2026-10-25).
-- pools/deviations keep their FK: they only ever carry real tickers.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS alert_log (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  alert_ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  alert_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  threshold_pct REAL,
  dev_at_alert_pct REAL,
  depth_at_alert_usd REAL,
  message_text TEXT,
  outcome TEXT,
  outcome_ts TEXT,
  closed_below_threshold_ts TEXT,
  -- channel ('en'/'id', fix ronde 6 S-new-10, 2026-11-08): one row per
  -- (alert, channel) delivery, so a failed channel is not suppressed
  -- by another channel's success. NULL = recorded before the column
  -- existed; dedup counts NULL rows as suppressing every channel.
  channel TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS poller_health (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  cycle_ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  cycles_expected INTEGER NOT NULL,
  cycles_ok INTEGER NOT NULL,
  failed_sources TEXT,
  gap_note TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS waitlist (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  contact TEXT,
  position_size_usd TEXT,
  venues TEXT,
  willing_price TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  consent INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);


===== KODE: migrations/2026-11-12-penambahan.sql =====
-- Migration 2026-11-12 (penambahan Radar, DESAIN_PENAMBAHAN_2026-11-12.md §3):
-- perp_snapshots (F1), liquidation_watch (F4), monday_gap (F2),
-- + exit_score / exit_score_version columns on deviations (F3).
-- Thresholds and formulas are PRE-REGISTERED in the design doc; this file
-- only creates the storage. Nothing here changes existing tables' rows.
--
-- APPLY ONLY WITH SEPTIAN ACC (do NOT run blindly). Order matters:
-- apply this migration to live Turso BEFORE deploying the poller that
-- writes these tables/columns — the new deviations INSERT references
-- exit_score, so a poller deployed first would fail every cycle.
--
-- Idempotency: CREATE TABLE / CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS run cleanly
-- twice. The two ALTERs FAIL if the column already exists — check first:
--   SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('deviations');
-- and run each ALTER only when its column is absent (same pattern as
-- migrations/2026-11-09-pools-excluded.sql).

-- F1 — Perp 3-leg snapshots: one row per ticker per 5-min cycle.
-- Only the 10 tickers with a live Hyperliquid XYZ perp (SPY/QQQ/GLD have
-- none — verified live 2026-11-12 — so they get NO rows, never a
-- fabricated price). funding / oi are NULL when the API does not
-- publish them; never fabricated.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS perp_snapshots (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  perp_px REAL,
  funding REAL,
  oi REAL,
  dex TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'xyz'
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_perp_ticker_ts ON perp_snapshots(ticker_id, ts);

-- F4 — Liquidation Guard: one row per ticker per hour in normal
-- conditions; extra rows every 5 min only while warning/critical.
-- liq_px_estimate is ALWAYS NULL until a public liquidation-level source
-- is verified (§2.4) — never fabricated. distance_pct is NULL with it.
-- There is deliberately NO oracle_lag_s column: the public Hyperliquid
-- info endpoints publish no oracle timestamp, so lag is unmeasurable
-- from them (see poller.py run_hourly_liquidation).
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS liquidation_watch (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  ts TEXT NOT NULL,
  oracle_px REAL,
  mark_px REAL,
  liq_px_estimate REAL,
  distance_pct REAL,
  source TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_liqwatch_ticker_ts ON liquidation_watch(ticker_id, ts);

-- F2 — Monday Gap Predictor: ONE row per ticker per week (Saturday-anchored
-- week_start). outcome stays 'pending' until Monday's actual gap is
-- computed from indexed deviations reads — never guessed.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS monday_gap (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ticker_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES universe(ticker_id),
  week_start TEXT NOT NULL,
  gap_pred_pct REAL NOT NULL,
  confidence REAL NOT NULL,
  method_version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'monday_gap_v1',
  outcome_gap_pct REAL,
  outcome TEXT,
  outcome_ts TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_monday_gap_ticker_week ON monday_gap(ticker_id, week_start);

-- F3 — Exit Score lives as COLUMNS on deviations (no new table, +0 writes:
-- the score rides the deviations row the cycle already writes).
-- Run ONLY if PRAGMA table_info(deviations) lacks the column.
ALTER TABLE deviations ADD COLUMN exit_score REAL;
ALTER TABLE deviations ADD COLUMN exit_score_version TEXT;


===== KODE: seed_multichain.py =====
"""Seed multichain universe + pools from verify_multichain_out.json.

Reads the LIVE verification output of verify_multichain.py and builds the
INSERTs for the three new issuer groups (Coinbase/Base, bStocks/BNB,
Robinhood/Robinhood Chain). Follows seed_universe.py (Solana xStocks)
conventions: idempotent DELETE-then-INSERT per ticker, only pools with
liquidity >= $100,000 at verification time are tracked (design §5.2).

Rules (design §1.1/§1.3, DESAIN_PENAMBAHAN_2026-11-12 §1.2):
- Only tickers with tier 1 or 2 (total >= $500k + >= 2 venues).
- At most CAP_PER_ISSUER tickers per issuer (default 13, the ACC'd cap),
  ranked by total liquidity at verification time. Tickers that pass the
  rule but fall outside the cap are listed as CAP-EXCLUDED, not seeded.
- A pool is seeded only if it is TRACKABLE: liquidity >= $100k AND the
  venue is a NAMED dex (dexId starting "0x" is an unnamed contract, not
  a verified venue — same lesson as communityV3) AND the quote token is
  not the base token itself (quote == symbol means an ambiguous /
  copycat quote; priceUsd cannot be trusted). Dropped pools are listed
  in the dry-run summary, not silently dropped.
- Ticker IDs: Coinbase keeps its DexScreener symbol (NVDAc), bStocks
  keeps its symbol (TSLAB), Robinhood takes its plain symbol + "r"
  (TSLAr) — the "r" suffix is a PROPOSAL (design doc fixes "c" and "B"
  but no Robinhood suffix); confirm before first live seed.
- Redemption / PoR rows are DOCUMENTATION, not live observation
  (design §3.5): all three issuers get status "unknown" with a note of
  the documented mechanism. No PoR feed is claimed for any of them.

SAFETY: --dry-run is the DEFAULT. It prints the SQL + summary and writes
nothing to Turso. --live executes via turso_client.pipeline ONLY when the
environment variable RADAR_SEED_CONFIRM is set to the exact value
"ACC-2026-11-12" (Septian's ACC for this seed, dated). Without it, --live
refuses. Never run --live without that ACC.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

sys.path.insert(0, '.')

LIQ_MIN = 100000.0
CAP_PER_ISSUER = 13
LIVE_CONFIRM = "ACC-2026-11-12"

ISSUER_META = {
    "coinbase": {
        "chain": "base",
        "redemption_note": (
            "Documented (riset 12 Nov): mint/redeem in-kind near-instant "
            "for authorised participants only; retail via secondary "
            "market. No public PoR found — backing via Alpaca custody + "
            "Chainlink price feeds (UNVERIFIED beyond that)."),
        "por_note": "unknown — no public PoR found (riset 12 Nov 2026).",
    },
    "bstocks": {
        "chain": "bsc",
        "redemption_note": (
            "Documented (riset 12 Nov): convert Binance brokerage share "
            "<-> bStock 1:1, zero fee, <1s (eligible users); on-chain "
            "mint/burn on deposit/redemption. Attestation published "
            "(detail UNVERIFIED)."),
        "por_note": ("unknown — attestation published per tracker "
                     "(detail UNVERIFIED); no live PoR feed checked."),
    },
    "robinhood": {
        "chain": "robinhood",
        "redemption_note": (
            "Documented (riset 12 Nov): redeemable for cash via "
            "authorised participants; redemption in underlying securities "
            "is roadmap / future date. No public PoR found."),
        "por_note": "unknown — no public PoR found (riset 12 Nov 2026).",
    },
}


def trackable_pools(candidate, max_pools=None):
    """Pools worth tracking: >= LIQ_MIN, named dex, quote != base symbol."""
    clean, dropped = [], []
    for p in candidate.get("pools", []):
        if p["liquidity_usd"] < LIQ_MIN:
            continue
        if (p["dex"] or "").startswith("0x"):
            dropped.append((p, "unnamed venue (dexId is a raw address)"))
            continue
        if p["quote"] == candidate["symbol"]:
            dropped.append((p, "quote token == base symbol (ambiguous)"))
            continue
        clean.append(p)
    clean.sort(key=lambda x: x["liquidity_usd"], reverse=True)
    if max_pools is not None and len(clean) > max_pools:
        dropped += [(p, f"beyond --max-pools-per-ticker {max_pools}")
                    for p in clean[max_pools:]]
        clean = clean[:max_pools]
    return clean, dropped


def build_statements(data, max_pools=None):
    """Return (statements, report) from verify_multichain_out.json data."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    verified_at = data.get("verified_at_utc", "?")
    by_issuer = {}
    for c in data.get("candidates", []):
        if c.get("tier") in (1, 2):
            by_issuer.setdefault(c["issuer"], []).append(c)
    stmts = []
    report = {"seeded": [], "cap_excluded": [], "failed": [],
              "dropped_pools": [], "issuers": sorted(by_issuer)}
    for issuer, cands in sorted(by_issuer.items()):
        cands.sort(key=lambda x: -x["total_liquidity_usd"])
        for c in cands[:CAP_PER_ISSUER]:
            sym = c["ticker_id"]
            chain = ISSUER_META[issuer]["chain"]
            token_json = json.dumps({chain: c["token_address"]})
            notes = (
                f"Live-verified {verified_at[:10]} via DexScreener "
                f"(verify_multichain.py): total_liq="
                f"${c['total_liquidity_usd']:,.0f}, "
                f"venues={c['venues']} (named {c.get('named_venues', '?')}), "
                f"token={c.get('token_name')}."
            )
            if c.get("truncated"):
                notes += (" NOTE: token-pairs hit the 30-pair API cap — "
                          "total is a lower bound.")
            stmts.append(("DELETE FROM pools WHERE ticker_id = ?", [sym]))
            stmts.append(("DELETE FROM universe WHERE ticker_id = ?", [sym]))
            stmts.append((
                "INSERT INTO universe (ticker_id, underlying, issuer, "
                "legal_type, tier, status, token_addresses, notes, "
                "created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                [sym, c["underlying"], issuer, "wrapper", c["tier"],
                 "active", token_json, notes, now, now],
            ))
            clean, dropped = trackable_pools(c, max_pools)
            for p in dropped:
                report["dropped_pools"].append(
                    (sym, p[0]["dex"], p[0]["pair_address"],
                     round(p[0]["liquidity_usd"]), p[1]))
            n_pools = 0
            for p in clean:
                pool_id = f"{p['chain']}:{p['pair_address']}"
                stmts.append((
                    "INSERT INTO pools (pool_id, ticker_id, chain, dex, "
                    "pair_address, quote_token, is_active, "
                    "min_liquidity_usd) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                    [pool_id, sym, p["chain"], p["dex"], p["pair_address"],
                     p["quote"], 1, LIQ_MIN],
                ))
                n_pools += 1
            report["seeded"].append(
                (sym, issuer, c["tier"], round(c["total_liquidity_usd"]),
                 c["venues"], n_pools,
                 round(sum(p["liquidity_usd"] for p in clean))))
        for c in cands[CAP_PER_ISSUER:]:
            report["cap_excluded"].append(
                (c["ticker_id"], issuer, c["tier"],
                 round(c["total_liquidity_usd"]),
                 "passes rule but outside top-13 cap for issuer"))
    for c in data.get("candidates", []):
        if not c.get("tier"):
            report["failed"].append(
                (c.get("ticker_id") or c["symbol"], c.get("issuer", "?"),
                 "; ".join(c.get("fail_reasons",
                                 [c.get("reason", "?")]))))
    # Documented (not live-observed) redemption + PoR rows per new issuer.
    for issuer in sorted(by_issuer):
        meta = ISSUER_META[issuer]
        stmts.append(
            ("DELETE FROM redemption_windows WHERE issuer = ?", [issuer]))
        stmts.append((
            "INSERT INTO redemption_windows (issuer, asset, status, "
            "window_note, observed_at, source, next_open_at) "
            "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            [issuer, "ALL", "unknown", meta["redemption_note"], now,
             "DESAIN §3.5 / riset 12 Nov (dokumentasi, bukan observasi live)",
             None],
        ))
        stmts.append((
            "INSERT INTO por_feeds (issuer, feed_address, last_update_ts, "
            "age_hours, reserves_vs_supply_note, checked_at) "
            "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            [issuer, None, None, None, meta["por_note"], now],
        ))
    return stmts, report


def render_sql(stmts):
    """Render (sql, args) pairs as readable SQL with literal values."""
    def lit(v):
        if v is None:
            return "NULL"
        if isinstance(v, bool):
            return "1" if v else "0"
        if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
            return repr(v)
        return "'" + str(v).replace("'", "''") + "'"
    lines = ["-- seed_multichain.py --dry-run output",
             "-- NO statements were executed. Review, then run with --live",
             "-- only after Septian's ACC (see module docstring).", ""]
    for sql, args in stmts:
        out, i = [], 0
        for ch in sql:
            if ch == "?" and args is not None and i < len(args):
                out.append(lit(args[i]))
                i += 1
            else:
                out.append(ch)
        lines.append("".join(out) + ";")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--input", default="verify_multichain_out.json",
                    help="verify_multichain.py output JSON "
                         "(default: %(default)s)")
    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",
                    help="print SQL + summary, write nothing (default)")
    ap.add_argument("--live", action="store_true",
                    help="execute against Turso (requires "
                         "RADAR_SEED_CONFIRM=ACC-2026-11-12 in env)")
    ap.add_argument("--max-pools-per-ticker", type=int, default=None,
                    help="cap tracked pools per ticker (default: all "
                         "trackable >= $100k pools)")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    with open(args.input) as f:
        data = json.load(f)
    stmts, report = build_statements(data, args.max_pools_per_ticker)
    n_tickers = len(report["seeded"])
    n_pools = sum(r[5] for r in report["seeded"])
    print(f"verified_at: {data.get('verified_at_utc', '?')}")
    print(f"tickers to seed: {n_tickers} "
          f"({len(report['cap_excluded'])} cap-excluded, "
          f"{len(report['failed'])} failed rule)")
    print(f"pools to track: {n_pools} "
          f"({len(report['dropped_pools'])} >= $100k pools dropped: "
          "unnamed venue / ambiguous quote / over cap)")
    for sym, issuer, tier, tot, venues, npl, qd in report["seeded"]:
        print(f"  SEED {sym:8s} {issuer:10s} T{tier} "
              f"total=${tot:>12,} venues={venues} "
              f"pools={npl} qualDepth=${qd:,}")
    for sym, issuer, tier, tot, why in report["cap_excluded"]:
        print(f"  CAP-EXCLUDE {sym:8s} {issuer:10s} T{tier} "
              f"total=${tot:>12,} — {why}")
    for sym, dex, pair, liq, why in report["dropped_pools"]:
        print(f"  DROP-POOL {sym:8s} {dex} {pair[:12]}… "
              f"${liq:>10,} — {why}")
    if args.live:
        if os.environ.get("RADAR_SEED_CONFIRM") != LIVE_CONFIRM:
            print("REFUSED: --live requires RADAR_SEED_CONFIRM="
                  f"{LIVE_CONFIRM} in the environment (Septian's ACC). "
                  "Nothing was written.", file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(2)
        from turso_client import pipeline, query
        pipeline(stmts)
        print("seeded OK (live)")
        print("universe:", query(
            "SELECT ticker_id, issuer, tier, status FROM universe "
            "WHERE issuer IN ('coinbase','bstocks','robinhood') "
            "ORDER BY issuer, ticker_id"))
        print("pools:", query(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM pools WHERE ticker_id IN "
            "(SELECT ticker_id FROM universe WHERE issuer IN "
            "('coinbase','bstocks','robinhood'))")[0])
    else:
        print()
        print(render_sql(stmts))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== KODE: verify_multichain.py =====
"""Verify the multichain Radar universe from LIVE DexScreener data.

Companion to verify_universe.py (Solana xStocks) — same binding rule
(design §1.1): total combined DEX liquidity >= $500,000 AND >= 2 distinct
DEX venues with liquidity > 0. Tier 1 additionally requires total >=
$1,000,000 AND 24h volume > 0 on >= 2 venues (design §1.3).

Groups covered (see SEED_MULTICHAIN_2026-11-12.md for verdicts):
  - coinbase_base:   Coinbase Tokenized Stocks on Base (suffix "c")
  - bstocks_bsc:     Binance bStocks on BNB Chain (suffix "B")
  - robinhood_chain: Robinhood Stock Tokens on Robinhood Chain (plain
                     symbols; proposed Radar ticker_id adds suffix "r")

Method (DexScreener public API, no key, curl via subprocess — urllib is
broken through the egress proxy, repo convention):
  1. DexScreener search per symbol, filtered to the group's chain and to
     pairs whose baseToken.symbol matches exactly.
  2. Group by base-token address; canonical token = largest total
     liquidity, PREFERING tokens that match the issuer hint
     (Coinbase: address starts 0xb200...; Robinhood: name contains
     "Robinhood Token"). The hint exists because largest-liquidity alone
     picks impostors: on 12 Nov 2026 the largest "MCD" token on Robinhood
     Chain was "Meridian Capital" ($829k), not McDonald's ($52k).
  3. DexScreener token-pairs for the canonical address, keeping ONLY
     base-side pairs (baseToken.address == canonical). The endpoint also
     returns pairs where the token is the QUOTE (e.g. MarsCoin/SPCXB,
     GLDSTN/GLD) — those price the OTHER token and must not count toward
     this token's liquidity. A first version of this script missed that
     filter and overstated totals by up to ~60% (SPYB looked Tier 2 at
     $778k; base-side only it is $354k — FAIL).

Caveats recorded per candidate:
  - truncated: token-pairs returns at most 30 pairs; when 30 are
    returned the total is a LOWER BOUND and a >= $100k pool could hide
    beyond the cut. Re-check with the pair endpoint if it matters.
  - KNOWN_ADDRESSES: tickers whose DexScreener search is unreliable
    (short/common symbols: JPM, V — search returns 30 pairs dominated by
    other chains / junk; MCD — impostor dominates). For these, the
    canonical address comes from yTokenize + a DexScreener name search
    and token-pairs is queried directly. Override only with a fresh
    source; never invent an address.
  - Unnamed venues: a dexId that is a raw 0x... address is NOT a named
    venue (same lesson as communityV3, VERIFIKASI_MULTICHAIN_2026-11-12
    §1e). Venue counts here still include them; the seed script drops
    pools on unnamed venues, and every passing ticker keeps >= 2 named
    venues without them.

Output: JSON (default verify_multichain_out.json in cwd, --out to
override) + human-readable table on stdout. Read-only: no DB writes,
no credentials. Safe to run weekly.
"""
import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone

SEARCH = "https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/search?q={}"
TOKENS = "https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/{}"

# Ticker -> (chain, issuer, underlying, proposed ticker_id) is derived
# from the group; only the symbol lists live here.
GROUPS = {
    "coinbase_base": {
        "chain": "base",
        "issuer": "coinbase",
        "suffix": "c",
        "symbols": ["NVDAc", "MSTRc", "SNDKc", "TSLAc", "AAPLc", "MSFTc",
                    "AMZNc", "GOOGLc", "METAc", "AMDc", "NFLXc", "AVGOc",
                    "JPMc", "CRCLc", "HOODc", "PLTRc", "COINc", "SPYc",
                    "QQQc", "GLDc", "BABAc", "DISc", "Vc", "PYPLc", "MCDc",
                    "UBERc", "XOMc", "COSTc", "WMTc", "BACc", "LLYc",
                    "UNHc", "ABBVc", "QCOMc", "TSMc", "INTCc", "SHOPc",
                    "SPOTc", "ARMc", "MRVLc", "NVOc", "GMc"],
    },
    "bstocks_bsc": {
        "chain": "bsc",
        "issuer": "bstocks",
        "suffix": "B",
        "symbols": ["TSLAB", "NVDAB", "SPYB", "SPCXB", "QQQB", "AAPLB",
                    "MSFTB", "AMZNB", "GOOGLB", "METAB", "NFLXB", "AMDB",
                    "AVGOB", "JPMB", "CRCLB", "HOODB", "PLTRB", "COINB",
                    "GLDB", "BABAB", "DISB", "VB", "PYPLB", "MCDB", "UBERB",
                    "XOMB", "COSTB", "WMTB", "BACB", "LLYB", "UNHB",
                    "ABBVB", "QCOMB", "TSMB", "INTCB", "SHOPB", "SPOTB",
                    "ARMB", "MRVLB", "NVOB", "GMB", "GDXB", "BMNRB"],
    },
    "robinhood_chain": {
        "chain": "robinhood",
        "issuer": "robinhood",
        "suffix": "r",  # proposed: plain DexScreener symbol + "r"
        "symbols": ["TSLA", "NVDA", "SPY", "JPM", "V", "PYPL", "MCD",
                    "DIS", "UBER", "GDX", "BMNR", "AAPL", "MSFT", "AMZN",
                    "GOOGL", "META", "NFLX", "AMD", "AVGO", "COIN", "HOOD",
                    "PLTR", "CRCL", "QQQ", "GLD", "BABA", "MSTR", "XOM",
                    "COST", "WMT", "BAC", "LLY", "UNH", "ABBV", "QCOM",
                    "TSM", "INTC", "SHOP", "SPOT", "ARM", "MRVL", "NVO"],
    },
}

# Canonical addresses for tickers whose DexScreener search is unreliable.
# Source: yTokenize stock pages + DexScreener name search, 12 Nov 2026.
# JPM: 0x07c44da0848960bff894f17584db8b2f60b2409e (yTokenize: "Robinhood
#   Chain: 0x07c4…409e", size $49.8K) — token-pairs: 0 pairs, $0.
# V:   0x9BFF87700337babF5099e99F322F888DafB1d551 ("Visa • Robinhood
#   Token", DexScreener search q=Visa) — token-pairs: 1 pair, $8,045.
# MCD: 0x2fdaa4604F10F4AE7f66fa48A174bCd25b1ece20 ("McDonald's •
#   Robinhood Token") — token-pairs: $52,231 base-side, 2 venues.
#   (Search canonical would be "Meridian Capital", $829k — impostor.)
KNOWN_ADDRESSES = {
    ("robinhood", "JPM"): "0x07c44da0848960bff894f17584db8b2f60b2409e",
    ("robinhood", "V"): "0x9BFF87700337babF5099e99F322F888DafB1d551",
    ("robinhood", "MCD"): "0x2fdaa4604F10F4AE7f66fa48A174bCd25b1ece20",
}


def fetch(url):
    r = subprocess.run(
        ["curl", "-s", "--max-time", "30", url],
        capture_output=True, text=True,
    )
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"curl failed for {url}: {r.stderr[:200]}")
    return json.loads(r.stdout)


def _is_dict(x):
    return isinstance(x, dict)


def issuer_hint_match(group, address, name):
    """True if this token looks like the group's issuer (not an impostor)."""
    if group == "coinbase_base":
        return (address or "").lower().startswith("0xb200")
    if group == "robinhood_chain":
        return "robinhood token" in (name or "").lower()
    return None  # bstocks: no reliable name/address hint; liq decides


def base_side_pairs(canonical_addr, chain):
    """All token-pairs where the canonical token is the BASE token.

    Returns (pools, n_returned, truncated). Pools are DexScreener pair
    dicts reduced to the fields the seed needs.
    """
    tp = fetch(TOKENS.format(canonical_addr))
    raw = tp.get("pairs") or []
    n_returned = len(raw)
    pools = []
    for pr in raw:
        if pr.get("chainId") != chain:
            continue
        bt = pr.get("baseToken") or {}
        if not _is_dict(bt):
            continue
        if (bt.get("address") or "").lower() != canonical_addr.lower():
            continue  # quote-side pair: prices the OTHER token, skip
        liq = (pr.get("liquidity") or {}).get("usd") or 0
        vol = (pr.get("volume") or {}).get("h24") or 0
        pools.append({
            "chain": pr.get("chainId"),
            "dex": pr.get("dexId"),
            "pair_address": pr.get("pairAddress"),
            "quote": (pr.get("quoteToken") or {}).get("symbol"),
            "price_usd": pr.get("priceUsd"),
            "liquidity_usd": liq,
            "volume_24h_usd": vol,
            "url": pr.get("url"),
        })
    pools.sort(key=lambda x: x["liquidity_usd"], reverse=True)
    return pools, n_returned, n_returned >= 30


def analyze(symbol, group_name, group):
    chain = group["chain"]
    base = {
        "symbol": symbol, "group": group_name, "chain": chain,
        "issuer": group["issuer"],
        "ticker_id": symbol if group["suffix"] in ("c", "B")
                     else symbol + group["suffix"],
        "underlying": symbol[:-1] if group["suffix"] in ("c", "B") else symbol,
    }
    warnings = []
    known = KNOWN_ADDRESSES.get((chain, symbol))
    if known:
        base["source"] = "known-address override (see KNOWN_ADDRESSES)"
        canonical_addr = known
        # token name comes from the pairs themselves
        pools, n_returned, truncated = base_side_pairs(canonical_addr, chain)
        token_name = None
        # fetch the token name from any pair's baseToken via token-pairs raw
        try:
            tp = fetch(TOKENS.format(canonical_addr))
            for pr in tp.get("pairs") or []:
                bt = pr.get("baseToken") or {}
                if _is_dict(bt) and (bt.get("address") or "").lower() \
                        == canonical_addr.lower():
                    token_name = bt.get("name")
                    break
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - name is nice-to-have
            pass
    else:
        data = fetch(SEARCH.format(symbol))
        pairs = data.get("pairs") or []
        by_token = {}
        for p in pairs:
            if p.get("chainId") != chain:
                continue
            bt = p.get("baseToken") or {}
            if not _is_dict(bt):
                continue
            if bt.get("symbol") != symbol:
                continue
            addr = bt.get("address")
            if not addr:
                continue
            by_token.setdefault(addr, {"name": bt.get("name"), "pairs": []})
            by_token[addr]["pairs"].append(p)
        if not by_token:
            return {**base, "found": False,
                    "reason": "no pairs with exact symbol on chain"}
        ranked = []
        for addr, info in by_token.items():
            tot = sum((pr.get("liquidity") or {}).get("usd") or 0
                      for pr in info["pairs"])
            ranked.append((addr, info["name"], tot, len(info["pairs"])))
        ranked.sort(key=lambda x: -x[2])
        hint_supported = group_name in ("coinbase_base", "robinhood_chain")
        hinted = ([r for r in ranked
                   if issuer_hint_match(group_name, r[0], r[1])]
                  if hint_supported else ranked)
        pick = hinted[0] if hinted else ranked[0]
        if hint_supported and not any(
                issuer_hint_match(group_name, r[0], r[1]) for r in ranked):
            warnings.append(
                "no token matched the issuer hint; canonical = largest "
                "liquidity — manual review advised (impostor risk)")
        if len(ranked) > 1:
            others = "; ".join(
                f"{name} ${tot:,.0f} ({n} pairs)"
                for _, name, tot, n in ranked[1:3])
            warnings.append(f"other same-symbol tokens on chain: {others}")
        canonical_addr, token_name = pick[0], pick[1]
        pools, n_returned, truncated = base_side_pairs(canonical_addr, chain)

    dexes = set()
    named_dexes = set()
    vol_venues = 0
    total = 0.0
    for p in pools:
        liq = p["liquidity_usd"]
        total += liq
        if liq > 0:
            dexes.add(p["dex"])
            if not (p["dex"] or "").startswith("0x"):
                named_dexes.add(p["dex"])
        if p["volume_24h_usd"] > 0:
            vol_venues += 1
    n_dex = len(dexes)
    tier = None
    reason = []
    if total < 500000:
        reason.append(f"total liquidity ${total:,.0f} < $500,000")
    if n_dex < 2:
        reason.append(f"only {n_dex} venue(s) with liquidity")
    if not reason:
        tier = 1 if (total >= 1000000 and vol_venues >= 2) else 2
    if truncated:
        warnings.append(
            "token-pairs returned 30 rows (API cap) — the base-side list "
            "may be incomplete, so the total is a LOWER BOUND; a "
            ">= $100k pool could hide beyond the cut")
    if len(named_dexes) < 2 and tier is not None:
        warnings.append(
            f"only {len(named_dexes)} NAMED venue(s) "
            f"({sorted(named_dexes)}) — address-dexIds excluded; "
            "re-check before seeding")
    return {
        **base, "found": True,
        "token_address": canonical_addr, "token_name": token_name,
        "total_liquidity_usd": round(total, 2),
        "venues": n_dex, "named_venues": len(named_dexes),
        "vol_venues": vol_venues,
        "n_pairs": len(pools), "pairs_returned": n_returned,
        "truncated": truncated, "tier": tier,
        "fail_reasons": reason, "warnings": warnings, "pools": pools,
    }


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="verify_multichain_out.json",
                    help="JSON output path (default: %(default)s)")
    ap.add_argument("--group", choices=list(GROUPS),
                    help="verify a single group only")
    ap.add_argument("--symbols", nargs="+",
                    help="verify only these symbols (within the group, "
                         "or across all groups if --group is omitted)")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    out = {"verified_at_utc": now, "candidates": []}
    groups = [args.group] if args.group else list(GROUPS)
    for gname in groups:
        group = GROUPS[gname]
        symbols = args.symbols or group["symbols"]
        for sym in symbols:
            if sym not in group["symbols"]:
                print(f"skip {sym}: not in {gname}", flush=True)
                continue
            try:
                res = analyze(sym, gname, group)
            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - report, don't crash
                res = {"symbol": sym, "group": gname, "chain": group["chain"],
                       "issuer": group["issuer"], "found": False,
                       "reason": f"fetch error: {e}"}
            out["candidates"].append(res)
            status = ("TIER " + str(res["tier"])) if res.get("tier") else (
                "EXCLUDE: " + "; ".join(res.get(
                    "fail_reasons", [res.get("reason", "?")])))
            warn = (" [WARN: " + " | ".join(res.get("warnings", [])) + "]"
                    if res.get("warnings") else "")
            print(f"{gname:16s} {sym:8s} "
                  f"liq=${res.get('total_liquidity_usd', 0):>14,.0f} "
                  f"venues={res.get('venues', 0)} "
                  f"vol_venues={res.get('vol_venues', 0)} "
                  f"pairs={res.get('n_pairs', 0)} -> {status}{warn}",
                  flush=True)
            time.sleep(0.35)
    with open(args.out, "w") as f:
        json.dump(out, f, indent=1)
    n_pass = sum(1 for c in out["candidates"] if c.get("tier"))
    print(f"passing: {n_pass}/{len(out['candidates'])} — wrote {args.out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== KODE: dashboard/risk.py =====
"""Weekend risk score v1 (design §5.5) — pure functions, no I/O.

Formula (documented interpretation where the design leaves a gap):
- deviation component: |dev_pct| * 10 points, capped at 40.
  Input is |dev_vs_median_pct| in median-only mode (vs-close is NULL
  until the data license is resolved); the caller MUST label this.
- depth component: 0 at qualifying depth >= $2.5M, 25 at <= $250k
  (the §5.2 alert floor), linear in between. Thinner = higher.
- redemption component: closed = 20, gated = 10, open = 0,
  unknown/None = NOT counted (excluded, not zeroed).
- PoR component: age >= 72h = 15, else linear 15 * age / 72.
  None = NOT counted (excluded, not zeroed).
Total = sum of available components; max_possible = sum of the maxima
of available components (65 when redemption + PoR are both unknown).
v3.2 normalizes to 0-100 (score_100 = total / max_possible * 100) so
tickers with different missing components are comparable — the raw
total / max_possible pair is kept for backward compatibility, and a
partial score always reports how many of the 4 components are present.
The score is ALWAYS labeled "experimental" until calibrated on >= 8
weekends (design §5.5). Method version: weekend_risk_v1.

Calibration counter (gap R8 P2-G1, built 2026-11-09): the "8
weekends" claim previously had no counter or state anywhere, so the
label could never transition on repository-visible evidence. The
counter below is the missing capability — it counts distinct
weekends (Saturday-anchored) with at least one observed data day,
from actual data dates. It does NOT change the score or its formula;
it only reports progress toward 8, and the label stays experimental
until the count reaches 8. Data starts 2026-10-24 (a Saturday), so
the first observed weekend is 2026-10-24/25.
"""
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta

METHOD_VERSION = "weekend_risk_v1"

DATA_START_DATE = date(2026, 10, 24)
CALIBRATION_WEEKENDS_REQUIRED = 8

DEPTH_FLOOR = 250_000.0
DEPTH_FULL = 2_500_000.0


def _clamp(x, lo, hi):
    return max(lo, min(hi, x))


def dev_component(dev_pct):
    if dev_pct is None:
        return None
    return round(_clamp(abs(dev_pct) * 10.0, 0.0, 40.0), 1)


def depth_component(depth_usd):
    if depth_usd is None:
        return None
    if depth_usd <= DEPTH_FLOOR:
        return 25.0
    if depth_usd >= DEPTH_FULL:
        return 0.0
    return round(25.0 * (1.0 - (depth_usd - DEPTH_FLOOR) / (DEPTH_FULL - DEPTH_FLOOR)), 1)


def redemption_component(status):
    mapping = {"closed": 20.0, "gated": 10.0, "open": 0.0}
    if status is None:
        return None
    return mapping.get(str(status).strip().lower())


def por_component(age_hours):
    if age_hours is None:
        return None
    if age_hours >= 72.0:
        return 15.0
    return round(15.0 * max(age_hours, 0.0) / 72.0, 1)


def weekend_risk_score(dev_pct, depth_usd, redemption_status, por_age_hours):
    """Returns dict with components, total, max_possible, the 0-100
    normalized score, component counts, and the partial flag."""
    comps = {
        "deviation": dev_component(dev_pct),
        "depth": depth_component(depth_usd),
        "redemption": redemption_component(redemption_status),
        "por": por_component(por_age_hours),
    }
    maxima = {"deviation": 40.0, "depth": 25.0, "redemption": 20.0, "por": 15.0}
    avail = {k: v for k, v in comps.items() if v is not None}
    total = round(sum(avail.values()), 1)
    max_possible = sum(maxima[k] for k in avail)
    score_100 = (round(total / max_possible * 100.0, 1)
                 if max_possible else None)
    return {
        "components": comps,
        "total": total,
        "max_possible": max_possible,
        "score_100": score_100,
        "components_present": len(avail),
        "components_total": 4,
        "partial": len(avail) < 4,
        "method_version": METHOD_VERSION,
    }


# --- Exit Score bands (design §2.3, display only) --------------------
# The score itself is computed by the poller and stored in
# deviations.exit_score + exit_score_version; this helper only maps a
# STORED score to its display band. Bands are labels, not advice:
# 0-39 "low exit pressure", 40-69 "medium", 70-100 "high". None (or a
# value outside 0-100) maps to None — a missing score is never shown
# as 0, and an impossible score is never banded.
def exit_score_band(score):
    if score is None:
        return None
    try:
        s = float(score)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    if s < 0 or s > 100:
        return None
    if s < 40:
        return "low"
    if s < 70:
        return "medium"
    return "high"


# --- Calibration counter (gap R8 P2-G1) --------------------------------
def _as_date(value):
    """Coerce a date / datetime / 'YYYY-MM-DD' (or ISO datetime) string
    to a date, or None when unparseable. Pure, never raises."""
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.date()
    if isinstance(value, date):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, str):
        text = value.strip()
        if not text:
            return None
        try:
            return datetime.fromisoformat(
                text.replace("Z", "+00:00")).date()
        except ValueError:
            pass
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(text[:10], "%Y-%m-%d").date()
        except ValueError:
            return None
    return None


def weekend_key(d):
    """The Saturday that starts the weekend containing date `d`
    (Saturday/Sunday only; returns None for weekdays)."""
    if d is None or d.weekday() < 5:
        return None
    return d - timedelta(days=d.weekday() - 5)


def calibration_progress(observed_dates, now=None):
    """Progress toward the >= 8 weekends calibration of the weekend
    risk score, counted from ACTUAL observed data dates (e.g. the
    distinct successful-cycle dates in poller_health).

    `observed_dates`: iterable of date / datetime / ISO-string days
    with data. A weekend counts when at least one Saturday or Sunday
    in it is observed; Sat+Sun of one weekend count as ONE weekend
    (weekend_days_observed reports the day count separately).

    Dates before DATA_START_DATE (no data can exist) and dates after
    `now` (future-dated rows must not inflate the count) are ignored,
    as are unparseable values. Never raises.

    Returns {"weekends_observed", "weekend_days_observed", "required",
    "remaining", "calibrated", "progress_pct", "data_start",
    "observed_weekends": [ISO Saturdays, sorted]}. The score stays
    experimental until calibrated is True; this function never changes
    the score itself.
    """
    now_d = _as_date(now) if now is not None else date.today()
    weekends = set()
    days = set()
    for raw in observed_dates or []:
        d = _as_date(raw)
        if d is None:
            continue
        if d < DATA_START_DATE:
            continue
        if now_d is not None and d > now_d:
            continue
        if d.weekday() < 5:
            continue
        days.add(d)
        key = d - timedelta(days=d.weekday() - 5)
        weekends.add(key)
    observed = sorted(weekends)
    n = len(observed)
    return {
        "weekends_observed": n,
        "weekend_days_observed": len(days),
        "required": CALIBRATION_WEEKENDS_REQUIRED,
        "remaining": max(CALIBRATION_WEEKENDS_REQUIRED - n, 0),
        "calibrated": n >= CALIBRATION_WEEKENDS_REQUIRED,
        "progress_pct": round(
            min(n / CALIBRATION_WEEKENDS_REQUIRED, 1.0) * 100.0, 1),
        "data_start": DATA_START_DATE.isoformat(),
        "observed_weekends": [d.isoformat() for d in observed],
    }


===== KODE: dashboard/logic.py =====
"""Pure dashboard logic (no Streamlit, no I/O) — unit-testable.

Freshness (design §6.3, unified contract 2026-11-08, fix ronde 5 M4):
stale when the last SUCCESSFUL poller cycle is older than 15 minutes
OR the last 2 cycles both failed. The age is anchored on the last
success, never on a failed cycle's timestamp — a failed cycle writes
a fresh-timestamped row, so anchoring on it would understate how old
the data really is. The same contract (age since last success,
failed_streak, stale) is exposed by /api/health and used by the public
frontend; the Telegram bot uses the same age with its own 30-min gate.
WITA = UTC + 8 (Asia/Makassar has no DST).
Best exit venue (design §6.1.8): deepest qualifying pool per size,
flagged when the size exceeds 5% of that pool's reserve. This is an
estimate from depth, never an execution promise.
"""
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

LIQ_MIN = 100000.0  # qualifying pool: reserve >= $100k (same as poller
# LIQ_MIN, bot.py LIQ_MIN, api/worker.js LIQ_MIN).

# NYSE holidays (full closes). Same set as poller.py / bot.py /
# web/src/lib/format.ts — qa/test_r8_fix.py S11 asserts all four
# identical. 2028 is the official NYSE Group calendar (announced
# 2025-12-23): 9 closures; New Year's Day 2028 falls on a Saturday
# and is NOT observed. Review annually; the S11 guard trips when the
# calendar no longer covers through the end of next year.
NYSE_HOLIDAYS = {
    "2026-01-01", "2026-01-19", "2026-02-16", "2026-04-03", "2026-05-25",
    "2026-06-19", "2026-07-03", "2026-09-07", "2026-11-26", "2026-12-25",
    "2027-01-01", "2027-01-18", "2027-02-15", "2027-03-26", "2027-05-31",
    "2027-06-18", "2027-07-05", "2027-09-06", "2027-11-25", "2027-12-24",
    "2028-01-17", "2028-02-21", "2028-04-14", "2028-05-29", "2028-06-19",
    "2028-07-04", "2028-09-04", "2028-11-23", "2028-12-25",
}

ET = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


def is_nyse_day(dt_et):
    return dt_et.weekday() < 5 and dt_et.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") not in NYSE_HOLIDAYS


def market_is_open(now_utc):
    """NYSE regular schedule, Mon–Fri 09:30–16:00 America/New_York.

    S12 (fix ronde 8): exchange holidays ARE tracked now (moved here
    from app.py so the pure logic is unit-testable). The old version
    was weekday-only, so on Thanksgiving the dashboard said "market
    open" while the bot's holiday-aware gate sent no spread alerts.
    """
    ny = now_utc.astimezone(ET)
    if not is_nyse_day(ny):
        return False
    mins = ny.hour * 60 + ny.minute
    return 9 * 60 + 30 <= mins < 16 * 60

WITA = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))
MONTHS_EN = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun",
             "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"]
MONTHS_ID = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "Mei", "Jun",
             "Jul", "Agu", "Sep", "Okt", "Nov", "Des"]


def parse_ts(iso):
    """Parse an ISO-8601 UTC timestamp ('...Z' or with offset) to aware UTC."""
    if iso is None:
        return None
    s = str(iso).strip()
    if s.endswith("Z"):
        s = s[:-1] + "+00:00"
    try:
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(s)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    if dt.tzinfo is None:
        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def fmt_wita(iso, lang="en"):
    """'24 Oct 2026 19:11 WITA' (en) / '24 Okt 2026 19:11 WITA' (id).

    The space between time and zone is a non-breaking space (U+00A0)
    so the two can never collapse or wrap apart in rendering ('23:36
    WITA' must read as one stamp, never '23:36WITA').
    """
    dt = parse_ts(iso)
    if dt is None:
        return "—"
    w = dt.astimezone(WITA)
    months = MONTHS_ID if lang == "id" else MONTHS_EN
    return (f"{w.day} {months[w.month - 1]} {w.year} "
            f"{w.hour:02d}:{w.minute:02d} WITA")


def freshness_status(health_rows, now_utc):
    """health_rows: newest-first list of {cycle_ts, cycles_ok}.

    Returns {last_ts, last_cycle_ts, last_success_ts, age_min,
    failed_streak, stale}. last_ts is the last SUCCESSFUL cycle's ts
    (the honest "last updated" stamp); last_cycle_ts is the newest row
    whatever its outcome. Empty input, or no success at all -> stale
    (no heartbeat / no trustworthy data).
    """
    if not health_rows:
        return {"last_ts": None, "last_cycle_ts": None, "last_success_ts": None,
                "age_min": None, "failed_streak": 0, "stale": True}
    last_cycle_ts = parse_ts(health_rows[0].get("cycle_ts"))
    streak = 0
    last_success = None
    for row in health_rows:
        try:
            ok = int(row.get("cycles_ok") or 0)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            ok = 0
        if ok == 1:
            last_success = parse_ts(row.get("cycle_ts"))
            break
        streak += 1
    if last_success is None:
        return {"last_ts": None,
                "last_cycle_ts": health_rows[0].get("cycle_ts"),
                "last_success_ts": None, "age_min": None,
                "failed_streak": streak, "stale": True}
    age_min = (now_utc - last_success).total_seconds() / 60.0
    # GG-15 (fix ronde 8, P2-M1 dashboard half): a last-success
    # timestamp in the FUTURE is stale, never fresh — a negative age
    # used to pass the `age_min > 15` test and read as fresh.
    stale = age_min < 0 or age_min > 15.0 or streak >= 2
    last_success_ts = None
    for row in health_rows:
        try:
            ok = int(row.get("cycles_ok") or 0)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            ok = 0
        if ok == 1:
            last_success_ts = row.get("cycle_ts")
            break
    return {"last_ts": last_success_ts,
            "last_cycle_ts": health_rows[0].get("cycle_ts"),
            "last_success_ts": last_success_ts, "age_min": age_min,
            "failed_streak": streak, "stale": stale}


def fmt_abbr(v):
    """'$1.40M' / '$312K' / '$2.38B' or '—' for None. KPI cards only —
    tables keep the full comma format (v3 rule).

    F2 (fix ronde 9, GG-17): a negative value renders U+2212, never
    ASCII hyphen-minus. The magnitude test already used abs(); the
    formatted value itself did not — '$-1.40M' until this fix.
    """
    if v is None:
        return "—"
    try:
        x = float(v)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "—"
    if abs(x) >= 1e9:
        s = f"${x / 1e9:,.2f}B"
    elif abs(x) >= 1e6:
        s = f"${x / 1e6:,.2f}M"
    elif abs(x) >= 1e3:
        s = f"${x / 1e3:,.0f}K"
    else:
        s = f"${x:,.0f}"
    return s.replace("-", "−")


PRICE_GUARD_MAX_DEV = 0.25  # price-sanity guard: drop prices >25%
# from the cross-venue median (same rule as the poller guard,
# bot.py guarded_venues, api/worker.js guardedPrices).


def guarded_prices(prices):
    """Prices after the 25%-from-median guard — the SAME rule as the
    poller guard (poller.py run_cycle), bot.py guarded_venues and
    api/worker.js guardedPrices: with >=3 prices, drop any price
    deviating >25% from the cross-venue median; with <3 prices no
    consensus exists, so the guard is skipped. If exclusion would
    empty the list, fall back to all prices. Pure: no I/O."""
    if len(prices) < 3:
        return list(prices)
    import statistics
    med = statistics.median(prices)
    if not med:
        return list(prices)
    kept = [p for p in prices
            if abs(p - med) / med <= PRICE_GUARD_MAX_DEV]
    return kept if kept else list(prices)


def cross_venue_spread(venues, dw_price):
    """(max - min qualifying venue price) / depth-weighted price, in %.

    Fix ronde 6 (M-new-1): the dashboard Streamlit app computed this
    WITHOUT the 25%-from-median guard the bot and Worker already use,
    so one bad tick on a qualifying pool (SPYx $0.1044, reserve
    $1.24M, 6 Nov 2026) showed ~100% here while bot/Worker showed
    0.168%. The spread is now computed on guarded_prices, mirroring
    api/worker.js crossVenueSpread exactly: qualifying filter
    (reserve >= LIQ_MIN) first, then the guard. Used by the master
    table and the ticker detail page (both go through this function).
    """
    prices = [float(v["price_usd"]) for v in venues
              if v.get("price_usd") is not None
              and (v.get("reserve_usd") or 0) >= LIQ_MIN]
    if len(prices) < 2 or not dw_price:
        return None
    guarded = guarded_prices(prices)
    return (max(guarded) - min(guarded)) / float(dw_price) * 100.0


def por_effective_age_hours(age_hours, last_update_ts, now_utc):
    """PoR age that cannot freeze below the truth (fix ronde 6,
    S-new-8): the stored age_hours is written by the hourly poller
    job, so if that job fails repeatedly the stored value freezes —
    possibly below the 72h alert threshold while last_update_ts is
    already older than 72h. The effective age is the max of the
    stored age and the age recomputed live from last_update_ts.
    Returns None when neither source yields an age."""
    eff = None
    try:
        eff = float(age_hours) if age_hours is not None else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        eff = None
    if last_update_ts:
        lu = parse_ts(last_update_ts)
        if lu is not None:
            live = (now_utc - lu).total_seconds() / 3600.0
            # GG-15 (fix ronde 8): a FUTURE last_update is not evidence
            # of age 0 — it is ignored, so the stored age stands (or
            # None stays None). The old code clamped it to 0.0.
            if live >= 0:
                if eff is None:
                    eff = max(0.0, live)
                else:
                    eff = max(eff, live)
    return eff


def best_exit_venues(venue_rows, sizes=(1000, 5000, 10000)):
    """venue_rows: latest snapshot per pool with reserve_usd + dex + price.

    Returns one row per size: deepest QUALIFYING pool (reserve_usd >=
    LIQ_MIN, $100k — fix ronde 5, M3), flagged when size > 5% of its
    reserve. No qualifying pool -> [] (caller shows 'no data'): a
    sub-$100k pool must never be crowned "best exit venue" while the
    Worker reports best_price null for the same ticker.
    """
    usable = [v for v in venue_rows
              if v.get("reserve_usd") is not None and v.get("price_usd") is not None
              and float(v["reserve_usd"]) >= LIQ_MIN]
    if not usable:
        return []
    best = max(usable, key=lambda v: float(v["reserve_usd"]))
    out = []
    for size in sizes:
        reserve = float(best["reserve_usd"])
        out.append({
            "size_usd": size,
            "dex": best.get("dex"),
            "chain": best.get("chain"),
            "price_usd": best.get("price_usd"),
            "reserve_usd": reserve,
            "depth_warning": size > 0.05 * reserve,
        })
    return out
