      clearInterval(id);
      clearInterval(tickId);
    };
  }, [load, initialData]);

  const toggleLang = (l: Lang) => {
    setLang(l);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("exit-radar-lang", l);
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  };

  const rows = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data) return [];
    const list = data.tickers.filter(
      (r) => tier === "all" || String(r.tier) === tier,
    );
    const by: Record<SortKey, (r: TickerRow) => number> = {
      dev: (r) => (r.dev_bps != null ? Math.abs(r.dev_bps) : -1),
      depth: (r) => (r.depth_usd ?? -1),
      name: (r) => 0,
      price: (r) => (r.dw_price ?? -1),
    };
    return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      if (sortKey === "name") {
        const c = a.ticker.localeCompare(b.ticker);
        return sortDir === "asc" ? c : -c;
      }
      const c = by[sortKey](a) - by[sortKey](b);
      return sortDir === "asc" ? c : -c;
    });
  }, [data, tier, sortKey, sortDir]);

  const worst = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data) return null;
    let w: TickerRow | null = null;
    for (const r of data.tickers) {
      // GG-30 (fix ronde 9): the verdict hero is a LIVE verdict — a
      // stale ticker must not set the worst deviation (M1 principle).
      if (r.dev_bps == null || r.stale) continue;
      if (!w || Math.abs(r.dev_bps) > Math.abs(w.dev_bps ?? 0)) w = r;
    }
    return w;
  }, [data]);

  // Coverage one-liner, built from LIVE /api/coverage data when it
  // loaded; the static coverage_short line is only the fallback when
  // the fetch failed (or has not returned yet). Never hardcodes the
  // "only Solana" claim — the template follows the live groups.
  const coverageShort = useMemo(() => {
    if (!coverage) return t(lang, "coverage_short");
    if (coverage.live.length === 1) {
      const g = coverage.live[0];
      return t(lang, "coverage_short_live", {
        tickers: g.tickers.length,
        issuer: g.issuer_display,
        pools: g.pools,
        chain: g.chain_display,
        dexes: g.dexes.map(cap).join(", "),
      });
    }
    if (coverage.live.length > 1) {
      const chains = [...new Set(coverage.live.map((g) => g.chain_display))];
      return t(lang, "coverage_short_multi", {
        tickers: coverage.live_tickers,
        n: chains.length,
        chains: chains.join(", "),
      });
    }
    return t(lang, "coverage_short");
  }, [coverage, lang]);

  /* v3.1 (opsi A, ACC Septian 25 Okt): DEX verdict from THREE signals, not
     deviation alone — a low deviation must not certify an exit when the
     spread is wide or depth is thin. Danger comes only from deviation
     (≥100 bps, the established tier); spread ≥1% and depth <$250k demote
     to caution at most — no invented thresholds. Missing signals are
     skipped, never zeroed. Redemption and PoR stay out of this verdict
     and are shown separately (stat strip below). */
  const worstSpread = useMemo(() => {
    let w: TickerRow | null = null;
    for (const r of data?.tickers ?? []) {
      // GG-30 (fix ronde 9): stale tickers do not set the verdict.
      if (r.spread_pct == null || r.stale) continue;
      if (!w || r.spread_pct > (w.spread_pct ?? 0)) w = r;
    }
    return w;
  }, [data]);
  const minDepth = useMemo(() => {
    let m: TickerRow | null = null;
    for (const r of data?.tickers ?? []) {
      // GG-30 (fix ronde 9): stale tickers do not set the verdict.
      if (r.depth_usd == null || r.stale) continue;
      if (!m || r.depth_usd < (m.depth_usd ?? Infinity)) m = r;
    }
    return m;
  }, [data]);
  const verdict = useMemo(() => {
    const wd = worst != null ? Math.abs(worst.dev_bps ?? 0) : null;
    if (wd == null && worstSpread == null && minDepth == null) return null;
    if (wd != null && wd >= 100) return "danger";
    if (
      (wd != null && wd >= 50) ||
      (worstSpread != null && (worstSpread.spread_pct ?? 0) >= 1) ||
      (minDepth != null && (minDepth.depth_usd ?? Infinity) < 250000)
    )
      return "caution";
    return "safe";
  }, [worst, worstSpread, minDepth]);
  const verdictText =
    verdict == null
      ? "—"
      : verdict === "safe"
        ? u(lang, "verdict_safe")
        : verdict === "caution"
          ? u(lang, "verdict_caution")
          : u(lang, "verdict_danger");
  /* Coverage honesty (QA 25 Okt): the verdict skips tickers without a
     deviation, so the headline itself carries the counted coverage. */
  const covN = data ? data.tickers.filter((r) => r.dev_bps != null && !r.stale).length : 0;
  const covTotal = data ? data.tickers.length : 0;
  const blips = useMemo(() => {
    const list = (data?.tickers ?? [])
      // P1-3 (fix ronde 9, GG-30): same M1 rule as the bar chart —
      // a stale ticker is NOT plotted as a live deviation.
      .filter((r) => r.dev_bps != null && !r.stale)
      .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.dev_bps ?? 0) - Math.abs(a.dev_bps ?? 0));
    /* Real deviations cluster near the center (all <50 bps on calm days),
       so the top-4 labels would collide. Keep at most 4 labels, dropping
       any label whose center lands within 45px of an already-placed one. */
    const placed: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
    return list.map((r, i) => {
      const a = Math.min(Math.abs(r.dev_bps ?? 0), 100);
      const rad = 22 + (a / 100) * 86;
      const ang = (i * 137.5 * Math.PI) / 180;
      const cx = 120 + rad * Math.cos(ang);
      const cy = 120 + rad * Math.sin(ang);
      const fill =
        r.status === "alert" ? "#f87171" : r.status === "watch" ? "#fb923c" : "#4ade80";
      const lx = Math.max(30, Math.min(210, cx));
      const ly = cy <= 120 ? cy + 18 : cy - 12;
      let label = i < 4;
      if (label) {
        for (const p of placed) {
          if (Math.hypot(lx - p.x, ly - p.y) < 45) {
            label = false;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (label) placed.push({ x: lx, y: ly });
      return { ticker: r.ticker, cx, cy, fill, label, lx, ly };
    });
  }, [data]);

  const maxDepth = useMemo(
    () => Math.max(0, ...(data?.tickers.map((r) => r.depth_usd ?? 0) ?? [0])),
    [data],
  );

  const latestTs = data?.meta.latest_ts ?? null;
  const ageMin = latestTs
    ? Math.round((Date.now() - Date.parse(latestTs)) / 60000)
    : null;
  // Freshness contract (M4): prefer the Worker's /api/health verdict;
  // /api/latest meta carries the same fields as a second fallback.
  // Snapshot age is only the last resort when health is unreachable.
  // GG-31 (fix ronde 9): a NEGATIVE age (future latest_ts) is stale,
  // never fresh — the old `ageMin > STALE_MIN` fallback let it read
  // as fresh, and the freshness text below read it as "just now".
  const healthStale = health?.stale ?? data?.meta.stale ?? null;
  const stale =
    healthStale != null ? healthStale : ageMin != null && (ageMin < 0 || ageMin > STALE_MIN);
  const staleTime = health?.last_success_ts ?? latestTs;

  // P1-2 (fix ronde 9, nit): redemption status + next-open come from
  // ONE find. Redemption is per-issuer in the data model (one row per
  // issuer; all 13 tickers share xStocks), so two independent finds
  // could only diverge if the model ever went per-asset — one find
  // removes the latent cross-pairing.
  const redemption = useMemo(() => {
    const found = data?.tickers.find(
      (r) => r.redemption_status != null || r.redemption_next_open != null,
    );
    return found ?? null;
  }, [data]);
  const redStatus = redemption?.redemption_status ?? null;
  const redNextOpen = redemption?.redemption_next_open ?? null;
  const porMaxAge = useMemo(() => {
    const ages = (data?.tickers ?? [])
      .map((r) => r.por_age_hours)
      .filter((a): a is number => a != null);
    return ages.length ? Math.max(...ages) : null;
  }, [data]);

  // M1 (fix ronde 7): a ticker whose own deviations row is stale is
  // NOT plotted as a live deviation, even when the poller as a whole
  // is fresh — it is listed separately below instead.
  const plotted = useMemo(
    () =>
      rows
        .filter((r) => r.dev_bps != null && !r.stale)
        .sort((a, b) => (b.dev_bps ?? 0) - (a.dev_bps ?? 0)),
    [rows],
  );
  const notPlotted = useMemo(
    () => rows.filter((r) => r.dev_bps == null && !r.stale).map((r) => r.ticker),
    [rows],
  );
  const staleTickers = useMemo(
    () => rows.filter((r) => r.stale).map((r) => r.ticker),
    [rows],
  );
  const barsRange = useMemo(() => {
    if (!plotted.length) return { lo: -100, hi: 100 };
    if (scale === "fixed") return { lo: -100, hi: 100 };
    const vals = plotted.map((r) => r.dev_bps ?? 0);
    return { lo: Math.min(...vals, 0) - 5, hi: Math.max(...vals, 0) + 5 };
  }, [plotted, scale]);
  const zeroPct =
    barsRange.hi === barsRange.lo
      ? 50
      : ((0 - barsRange.lo) / (barsRange.hi - barsRange.lo)) * 100;

  const toggleSort = (key: SortKey) => {
    if (sortKey === key) {
      setSortDir((d) => (d === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc"));
    } else {
      setSortKey(key);
      setSortDir(key === "name" ? "asc" : "desc");
    }
  };

  const sortHead = (key: SortKey, label: string, thClass?: string) => {
    const active = sortKey === key;
    return (
      <th
        className={thClass}
        aria-sort={active ? (sortDir === "asc" ? "ascending" : "descending") : "none"}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          className="sortbtn"
          onClick={() => toggleSort(key)}
          aria-label={
            active ? `${label}, ${u(lang, sortDir === "asc" ? "dir_asc" : "dir_desc")}` : label
          }
        >
          {label} {active && <span className="ind">{sortDir === "asc" ? "▲" : "▼"}</span>}
        </button>
      </th>
    );
  };

  const downloadCsv = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([buildCsv(rows)], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = u(lang, "csv_filename");
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const freshnessText = latestTs
    ? ageMin != null && ageMin === 0
      ? t(lang, "freshness_now", { time: fmtWITA(latestTs, lang) })
      : // GG-31 (fix ronde 9): a future latest_ts (negative age) makes
        // NO freshness claim — the bare timestamp, never "just now".
        ageMin != null && ageMin < 0
        ? fmtWITA(latestTs, lang)
        : t(lang, "freshness", { time: fmtWITA(latestTs, lang), mins: ageMin ?? 0 })
    : t(lang, "loading");

  return (
    <div className="wrap">
      <Header lang={lang} onLangChange={toggleLang} active="home" right={<span>{freshnessText}</span>} />

      {stale && staleTime && (
        <div className="banner" role="status">
          {t(lang, "stale_banner", { time: fmtWITA(staleTime, lang) })}
        </div>
      )}
      {/* S-new-7 (fix ronde 6): stale with NO timestamp at all (no
          successful cycle ever, no deviation rows yet) still gets a
          banner — a generic one without a time, instead of silence. */}
      {stale && !staleTime && (
        <div className="banner" role="status">
          {t(lang, "stale_banner_notime")}
        </div>
      )}
      {error && data && (
        <div className="errorbox" role="alert">
          {u(lang, "load_error")}{" "}
          <button type="button" onClick={load}>
            {u(lang, "retry")}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {!data && loading && (
        <div aria-busy="true" aria-label={t(lang, "loading")}>
          <div className="scope-hero" aria-hidden="true">
            <div className="skel" style={{ width: 240, height: 240, borderRadius: "50%" }} />
            <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <div className="skel" style={{ width: 160 }} />
              <div className="skel" style={{ width: 220, height: 30, margin: "10px 0" }} />
              <div className="skel" style={{ width: 200, height: 14 }} />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="statstrip" aria-hidden="true">
            <div className="skel" style={{ width: 140, height: 14 }} />
            <div className="skel" style={{ width: 120, height: 14 }} />
            <div className="skel" style={{ width: 160, height: 14 }} />
          </div>
          <p className="hint">{t(lang, "loading")}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {data && (
        <>
          <div className="verdict-hero">
            <div className="scope-bg">
              <svg
                width="240"
                height="240"
                viewBox="0 0 240 240"
                role="img"
                aria-label={u(lang, "scope_label")}
              >
                <circle cx="120" cy="120" r="112" fill="none" stroke="#fbbf24" strokeWidth="1.5" opacity=".8" />
                <circle cx="120" cy="120" r="78" fill="none" stroke="#8ba190" strokeWidth="1" opacity=".5" />
                <circle cx="120" cy="120" r="44" fill="none" stroke="#8ba190" strokeWidth="1" opacity=".5" />
                <line x1="120" y1="8" x2="120" y2="232" stroke="#8ba190" strokeWidth="1" opacity=".35" />
                <line x1="8" y1="120" x2="232" y2="120" stroke="#8ba190" strokeWidth="1" opacity=".35" />
                <g className="sweep">
                  <path d="M120 120 L120 14 A106 106 0 0 1 195 45 Z" fill="#fbbf24" opacity=".28" />
                  <line x1="120" y1="120" x2="120" y2="14" stroke="#fbbf24" strokeWidth="2" />
                </g>
                <circle cx="120" cy="120" r="4" fill="#fbbf24" />
                {blips.map((b) => (
                  <g key={b.ticker}>
                    <circle cx={b.cx} cy={b.cy} r={b.label ? 6 : 4} fill={b.fill} />
                    {b.label && (
                      <text
                        x={b.lx}
                        y={b.ly}
                        textAnchor="middle"
                        fill={b.fill}
                        fontSize="11"
                        fontFamily="JetBrains Mono, monospace"
                      >
                        {b.ticker}
                      </text>
                    )}
                  </g>
                ))}
              </svg>
            </div>
            <div className="verdict">
              <div className="lbl">{u(lang, "verdict_label")}</div>
              <div className={`vtext ${verdict ?? ""}`}>
                {verdictText}
                {verdict != null && (
                  <span className="vcount">
                    {" "}
                    {u(lang, "verdict_headline_count")
                      .replace("{n}", String(covN))
                      .replace("{total}", String(covTotal))}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="worst">
                {worst
                  ? `${u(lang, "verdict_dev_worst")}: ${worst.ticker} ${fmtSigned1(worst.dev_bps)} bps`
                  : "—"}
              </div>
              <div className="verdict-cards">
                <div className="verdict-card">
                  <div className="k">{u(lang, "verdict_dev_worst")}</div>
                  <div className="v">
                    {worst ? `${worst.ticker} ${fmtSigned1(worst.dev_bps)} bps` : "—"}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="verdict-card">
                  <div className="k">{u(lang, "verdict_spread_worst")}</div>
                  <div className="v">
                    {worstSpread
                      ? `${worstSpread.ticker} ${worstSpread.spread_pct?.toFixed(2)}%`
                      : "—"}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="verdict-card">
                  <div className="k">{u(lang, "verdict_depth_min")}</div>
                  <div className="v">
                    {minDepth ? `${minDepth.ticker} ${fmtDepth(minDepth.depth_usd)}` : "—"}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="rule">{u(lang, "verdict_rule")}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="statstrip">
            <span>
              {data.tickers.length} {t(lang, "nav_ticker")} ·{" "}
              <b>
                {data.meta.venues_live}/{data.meta.venues_total}
              </b>{" "}
              {t(lang, "kpi_venues_sub")}
            </span>
            <span>
              {t(lang, "kpi_por")}:{" "}
              <b>{porMaxAge != null ? fmtAge(porMaxAge) : "—"}</b>{" "}
              {porMaxAge != null
                ? /* Fresh/stale boundary = bot PoR alert threshold (72h). */
                  `${porMaxAge > 72 ? u(lang, "por_age_stale") : u(lang, "por_age_fresh")} ${u(lang, "por_stale_sub")}`
                : t(lang, "kpi_por_sub")}
            </span>
            <span>
              {t(lang, "kpi_redemption")}: <b>{redLabel(lang, redStatus)}</b>{" "}
              {redStatus === "paused"
                ? t(lang, "kpi_redemption_sub_paused")
                : redStatus != null
                  ? t(lang, "kpi_redemption_sub_live")
                  : t(lang, "kpi_redemption_sub")}
              {redNextOpen && redStatus !== "open" && (
                <>
                  {" · "}
                  {t(lang, "next_open_label")}: {fmtWITA(redNextOpen, lang)}
                </>
              )}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="cover">
            <b>{t(lang, "coverage_live_line")}</b>
            <br />
            {coverageShort}
          </div>

          {/* Coverage section (2026-11-12): the banner is derived from
              live data — "only xStocks on Solana" shows only while
              /api/coverage reports exactly that, and flips by itself
              when a new chain's pools are seeded and polled. The live
              table lists what is actually polled; the pending table
              lists the dated 12 Nov 2026 verification verdicts. */}
          {coverage && (
            <>
              {coverage.only_solana_xstocks ? (
                <div className="banner" role="status">
                  {t(lang, "coverage_banner_only")}
                </div>
              ) : coverage.live.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="banner" role="status">
                  {t(lang, "coverage_banner_multi", {
                    n: new Set(coverage.live.map((g) => g.chain)).size,
                  })}
                </div>
              ) : null}
              <h2 className="sec">{t(lang, "coverage_table_title")}</h2>
              <p className="hint">{t(lang, "coverage_table_note")}</p>
              {coverage.live.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="tblwrap">
                  <table>
                    <thead>
                      <tr>
                        <th>{t(lang, "col_chain")}</th>
                        <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_issuer")}</th>
                        <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_platform")}</th>
                        <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_tokens")}</th>
                        <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_pools")}</th>
                        <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_status")}</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {coverage.live.map((g) => (
                        <tr key={`${g.chain}-${g.issuer}`}>
                          <td>{g.chain_display}</td>
                          <td>{g.issuer_display}</td>
                          <td>{g.dexes.map(cap).join(", ")}</td>
                          <td>{g.tickers.join(", ")}</td>
                          <td className="num">{g.pools}</td>
                          <td>{t(lang, "cov_status_live")}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <p className="hint">{t(lang, "no_data")}</p>
              )}
              <p>
                <b>{t(lang, "cov_pending_title")}</b>
              </p>
              <p className="hint">{t(lang, "cov_pending_note")}</p>
              <div className="tblwrap">
                <table>
                  <thead>
                    <tr>
                      <th>{t(lang, "col_chain")}</th>
                      <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_issuer")}</th>
                      <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_platform")}</th>
                      <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_tokens")}</th>
                      <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_status")}</th>
                      <th>{t(lang, "cov_col_reason")}</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {coverage.pending.map((p) => (
                      <tr key={`${p.chain}-${p.issuer}`}>
                        <td>{p.chain_display}</td>
                        <td>{p.issuer_display}</td>
                        <td>{p.platforms}</td>
                        <td>{p.tokens}</td>
                        <td>{t(lang, "cov_status_" + p.verdict)}</td>
                        <td>{t(lang, p.reason_key)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          <h2 className="sec">{t(lang, "heatmap_title")}</h2>
          <div className="controls">
            <span className="lbl">{t(lang, "sort_label")}:</span>
            <span className="seg" role="group" aria-label={t(lang, "sort_label")}>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={sortKey === "dev"}
                onClick={() => {
                  setSortKey("dev");
                  setSortDir("desc");
                }}
              >
                {t(lang, "sort_dev")}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={sortKey === "depth"}
                onClick={() => {
                  setSortKey("depth");
                  setSortDir("desc");
                }}
              >
                {t(lang, "sort_depth")}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={sortKey === "name"}
                onClick={() => {
                  setSortKey("name");
                  setSortDir("asc");
                }}
              >
                {t(lang, "sort_name")}
              </button>
            </span>
            <span className="lbl">{t(lang, "tier_filter")}:</span>
            <span className="seg" role="group" aria-label={t(lang, "tier_filter")}>
              <button type="button" aria-pressed={tier === "all"} onClick={() => setTier("all")}>
                {t(lang, "tier_all")}
              </button>
              <button type="button" aria-pressed={tier === "1"} onClick={() => setTier("1")}>
                Tier 1
              </button>
              <button type="button" aria-pressed={tier === "2"} onClick={() => setTier("2")}>
                Tier 2
              </button>
            </span>
            <button type="button" className="refresh" onClick={load} disabled={loading}>
              {loading ? t(lang, "refresh_updating") : t(lang, "refresh_button")}
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="refresh" onClick={downloadCsv}>
              {t(lang, "download_csv")}
            </button>
          </div>
          <p className="hint">
            {t(lang, "home_table_note_cycle")} {t(lang, "home_table_hint")}{" "}
            {u(lang, "auto_refresh")}.
          </p>

          <div className="tblwrap home-tbl">
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  {sortHead("name", t(lang, "col_ticker"))}
                  <th className="hide-s">{t(lang, "col_tier")}</th>
                  {sortHead("price", t(lang, "dw_price"))}
                  {sortHead("dev", t(lang, "col_dev_bps"))}
                  <th className="hide-s">{t(lang, "col_spread")}</th>
                  {sortHead("depth", t(lang, "col_depth10k"))}
                  <th className="hide-s">{t(lang, "redemption_title")}</th>
                  <th className="hide-s">{t(lang, "por_age_label")}</th>
                  <th className="hide-s">{t(lang, "exit_score_col")}</th>
                  <th>{t(lang, "col_status")}</th>
                  <th>{t(lang, "detail_link")}</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {rows.map((r) => {
                  const alpha =
                    r.dev_bps != null ? Math.min(Math.abs(r.dev_bps) / 100, 0.5) : 0;
                  const tint =
                    r.dev_bps != null ? { background: `rgba(224,123,74,${alpha.toFixed(2)})` } : {};
                  return (
                    <tr key={r.ticker}>
                      <td className="tk">
                        <StatusBlip status={r.status} />
                        <Link href={`/ticker/${r.ticker}`}>
                          <b>{r.ticker}</b>
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                      <td className="hide-s">{r.tier ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="num">{fmtUsd(r.dw_price)}</td>
                      <td
                        className="num dev"
                        style={tint}
                        title={r.dev_bps == null ? t(lang, "dash_no_median") : undefined}
                      >
                        {fmtSigned1(r.dev_bps)}
                        <DevBar devBps={r.dev_bps} status={r.status} />
                      </td>
                      <td
                        className="num hide-s"
                        title={r.spread_pct == null ? t(lang, "dash_no_median") : undefined}
                      >
                        {fmtPct(r.spread_pct)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="num">
                        <div>{fmtDepth(r.depth_usd)}</div>
                        <div className="dbar" style={{ marginLeft: "auto", marginTop: "4px" }}>
                          <i
                            style={{
                              width: `${maxDepth > 0 ? ((r.depth_usd ?? 0) / maxDepth) * 100 : 0}%`,
                            }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="hide-s">
                        <span className={`pill ${redPill(r.redemption_status)}`}>
                          {redLabel(lang, r.redemption_status)}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="dim hide-s">
                        {r.por_age_hours != null
                          ? fmtAge(r.por_age_hours)
                          : t(lang, "por_not_published")}
                      </td>
                      <td className="num hide-s">
                        {r.exit_score != null ? r.exit_score.toFixed(1) : "—"}
                      </td>
                      <td title={r.status === "na" ? t(lang, "dash_no_median") : undefined}>
                        <span className={`pill ${r.status}`}>
                          {statusLabel(lang, r.status)}
                        </span>
                        {/* M1 (fix ronde 7): per-ticker staleness flag. */}
                        {r.stale && (
                          <span className="pill stale"> {t(lang, "ticker_stale")}</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <Link className="detailbtn" href={`/ticker/${r.ticker}`} aria-label={`${t(lang, "detail_link")} ${r.ticker}`}>
                          {t(lang, "detail_link")} →
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          <p className="hint">{t(lang, "status_note")}</p>
          {!rows.some((r) => r.exit_score != null) && (
            <p className="hint">{t(lang, "exit_score_col_note")}</p>
          )}
          <div className="rowcards">
            {rows.map((r) => (
              <div className="rc" key={r.ticker}>
                <div className="top">
                  <span>
                    <StatusBlip status={r.status} />
                    <Link href={`/ticker/${r.ticker}`}>
                      <b>{r.ticker}</b>
                    </Link>
                  </span>
                  <span className={`pill ${r.status}`}>
                    {statusLabel(lang, r.status)}
                  </span>
                  {/* GG-30 (fix ronde 9): the row card shows the stale
                      pill, same as the table row. */}
                  {r.stale && (
                    <span className="pill stale"> {t(lang, "ticker_stale")}</span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="mid">
                  <span className="num">{fmtUsd(r.dw_price)}</span>
                  <span className="spark">
                    <Sparkline series={r.spark_bps} status={r.status} />
                  </span>
                  <span className="num">
                    <b>{fmtSigned1(r.dev_bps)} bps</b>
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="mid">
                  <span className="dim">
                    {t(lang, "col_depth10k")}: {fmtDepth(r.depth_usd)}
                  </span>
                  <Link className="detailbtn" href={`/ticker/${r.ticker}`} aria-label={`${t(lang, "detail_link")} ${r.ticker}`}>
                    {t(lang, "detail_link")} →
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          {rows.length === 0 && <p className="hint">{t(lang, "no_data")}</p>}

          <h2 className="sec">{t(lang, "bars_title")}</h2>
          <div className="controls">
            <span className="lbl">{t(lang, "bars_scale_label")}:</span>
            <span className="seg" role="group" aria-label={t(lang, "bars_scale_label")}>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={scale === "zoomed"}
                onClick={() => setScale("zoomed")}
              >
                {t(lang, "bars_scale_zoomed")}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={scale === "fixed"}
                onClick={() => setScale("fixed")}
              >
                {t(lang, "bars_scale_fixed")}
              </button>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="chart">
            {plotted.map((r) => {
              const v = r.dev_bps ?? 0;
              const pct = ((v - barsRange.lo) / (barsRange.hi - barsRange.lo)) * 100;
              const left = v < 0 ? pct : zeroPct;
              const width = v < 0 ? zeroPct - pct : pct - zeroPct;
              return (
                <div className="brow" key={r.ticker}>
                  <span className="bt">{r.ticker}</span>
                  <span className="btrack">
                    <i
                      className={r.status === "na" ? "" : r.status}
                      style={{ left: `${left.toFixed(1)}%`, width: `${Math.max(width, 0).toFixed(1)}%` }}
                    />
                    <b className="zero" style={{ left: `${zeroPct.toFixed(1)}%` }} />
                  </span>
                  <span className="bv">{fmtSigned1(r.dev_bps)}</span>
                </div>
              );
            })}
            <div className="axis">
              <span>{Math.round(barsRange.lo)} bps</span>
              <span>0</span>
              <span>{Math.round(barsRange.hi)} bps</span>
            </div>
            <p className="hint" style={{ margin: "10px 0 0" }}>
              {t(lang, "bars_note")}{" "}
              {t(lang, "bars_range", {
                lo: fmtSigned1(barsRange.lo),
                hi: fmtSigned1(barsRange.hi),
              })}
              {scale === "fixed" &&
                plotted.some((r) => Math.abs(r.dev_bps ?? 0) > 100) && (
                  <>
                    {" "}
                    {t(lang, "bars_clipped", {
                      tickers: plotted
                        .filter((r) => Math.abs(r.dev_bps ?? 0) > 100)
                        .map((r) => r.ticker)
                        .join(", "),
                    })}
                  </>
                )}
              {notPlotted.length > 0 && (
                <>
                  <br />
                  {t(lang, "bars_nodata")} {notPlotted.join(", ")}.
                </>
              )}
              {staleTickers.length > 0 && (
                <>
                  <br />
                  {t(lang, "bars_stale")} {staleTickers.join(", ")}.
                </>
              )}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="tg-card">
            <svg className="tg-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2.01 21 23 12 2.01 3 2 10l15 2-15 2z" /></svg>
            <div className="tg-text">
              <b>{u(lang, "tg_card_title")}</b>
              <p>{u(lang, "tg_card_note")}</p>
              <div className="tg-btns">
                <a className="btn tg-btn" href="https://t.me/exitradar_en" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">{u(lang, "tg_ch_en")}</a>
                <a className="btn tg-btn" href="https://t.me/exitradar_in" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">{u(lang, "tg_ch_id")}</a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="foot-sweep" aria-hidden="true">
            <svg width="100%" height="14" viewBox="0 0 1180 14" preserveAspectRatio="none">
              <path
                d="M0 14 Q 590 -10 1180 14"
                fill="none"
                style={{ stroke: "var(--accent)" }}
                strokeWidth="1.5"
                opacity=".7"
              />
            </svg>
          </div>
          <div className="foot">
            {u(lang, "method_short")}
            <br />
            {t(lang, "disclaimer")} {t(lang, "footer_note")}
            <br />
            <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/in/septian-panjaya">
              {t(lang, "linkedin_label")}
            </a>
            <br />
            Telegram: <a href="https://t.me/exitradar_en">EN</a> · <a href="https://t.me/exitradar_in">ID</a>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {!data && error && (
        <div className="errorbox" role="alert">
          {u(lang, "load_error")}{" "}
          <button type="button" onClick={load}>
            {u(lang, "retry")}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


===== KODE: web/src/lib/api.ts =====
/* Types for the radar-api Worker (see ../api/worker.js). */

export type TickerRow = {
  ticker: string;
  tier: number | null;
  issuer: string;
  dw_price: number | null;
  dev_pct: number | null;
  dev_bps: number | null;
  // M1 (fix ronde 7): per-ticker freshness — the row's own deviations
  // timestamp, not just the global poller verdict in meta.
  dev_ts: string | null;
  dev_age_min?: number | null;
  stale: boolean;
  best_price: number | null;
  best_dex: string | null;
  spread_pct: number | null;
  depth_usd: number | null;
  pools_used: number | null;
  // Gap R9: the Worker returns pools_excluded per row (loadLatest);
  // null when the column is absent from the DB.
  pools_excluded: number | null;
  // Exit Score (exit_score_v1) + perp 3-leg (perp_3leg_v1), 2026-11-12.
  // All null until the migration/poller writes the data — null means
  // "not available yet", never 0.
  exit_score: number | null;
  exit_score_version: string | null;
  perp_px: number | null;
  perp_dev_pct: number | null;
  perp_funding: number | null;
  perp_oi: number | null;
  perp_ts: string | null;
  redemption_status: string | null;
  redemption_next_open: string | null;
  redemption_note_en: string | null;
  por_age_hours: number | null;
  por_last_update: string | null;
  spark_bps: number[];
  change_1h_bps: number | null;
  status: "ok" | "watch" | "alert" | "na";
};

export type LatestResponse = {
  tickers: TickerRow[];
  meta: {
    latest_ts: string | null;
    venues_live: number;
    venues_total: number;
    poller_last_cycle: string | null;
    // Freshness contract (fix ronde 5, M4): mirrored from /api/health.
    cycles_ok?: number | null;
    failed_streak?: number;
    last_success_ts?: string | null;
    stale?: boolean;
    fresh?: boolean;
    // S15 (fix ronde 8): the Worker returns stale_tickers in meta.
    stale_tickers?: string[];
  };
};

export async function fetchLatest(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<LatestResponse> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RADAR_API_BASE;
  if (!base) throw new Error("API base URL is not configured");
  const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/latest`, { cache: "no-store", signal });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`API HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return (await res.json()) as LatestResponse;
}

export type HealthResponse = {
  latest_ts: string | null;
  poller_last_cycle: string | null;
  age_minutes: number | null;
  fresh: boolean;
  stale: boolean;
  cycles_ok: number | null;
  failed_streak: number;
  last_success_ts: string | null;
  venues_live: number;
  venues_total: number;
};

/* Freshness contract (fix ronde 5, M4): the homepage banner uses the
   Worker's /api/health verdict (age since last SUCCESSFUL cycle +
   failed streak), not a locally computed snapshot age. */
export async function fetchHealth(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<HealthResponse> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RADAR_API_BASE;
  if (!base) throw new Error("API base URL is not configured");
  const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/health`, { cache: "no-store", signal });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`API HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return (await res.json()) as HealthResponse;
}

export type AlertRow = {
  ticker: string;
  ts: string;
  type: string;
  // S14 (fix ronde 8): the Worker returns the delivery channel per
  // feed row (since R7); the type now declares it.
  channel: string | null;
  figure: number | null;
};

export type AlertsResponse = {
  alerts: AlertRow[];
};

export async function fetchAlerts(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<AlertsResponse> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RADAR_API_BASE;
  if (!base) throw new Error("API base URL is not configured");
  const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/alerts`, { cache: "no-store", signal });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`API HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return (await res.json()) as AlertsResponse;
}

export type VenueRow = {
  dex: string;
  chain: string;
  pair_address: string | null;
  price_usd: number | null;
  reserve_usd: number | null;
  volume_24h_usd: number | null;
  ts: string | null;
  qualifies: boolean;
};

export type TickerDetail = {
  ticker: string;
  issuer: string;
  underlying: string | null;
  legal_type: string | null;
  tier: number | null;
  dw_price: number | null;
  dev_pct: number | null;
  dev_bps: number | null;
  // M1 (fix ronde 7): per-ticker freshness + S9: the canonical global
  // health verdict, carried by the detail response itself.
  dev_ts: string | null;
  dev_age_min: number | null;
  stale: boolean;
  health_stale: boolean | null;
  health_fresh: boolean | null;
  health_last_success_ts: string | null;
  health_failed_streak: number | null;
  best_price: number | null;
  best_dex: string | null;
  spread_pct: number | null;
  depth_usd: number | null;
  pools_used: number | null;
  pools_excluded: number;
  // Exit Score (exit_score_v1) + perp 3-leg (perp_3leg_v1), 2026-11-12.
  // All null until the migration/poller writes the data — null means
  // "not available yet", never 0.
  exit_score: number | null;
  exit_score_version: string | null;
  perp_px: number | null;
  perp_dev_pct: number | null;
  perp_funding: number | null;
  perp_oi: number | null;
  perp_ts: string | null;
  redemption_status: string | null;
  redemption_next_open: string | null;
  redemption_note_en: string | null;
  redemption_note: string | null;
  redemption_observed_at: string | null;
  redemption_source: string | null;
  por_age_hours: number | null;
  por_last_update: string | null;
  por_feed_address: string | null;
  por_note: string | null;
  por_shares_held: string | null;
  por_supply: string | null;
  por_token_last_update: string | null;
  spark_bps: number[];
  change_1h_bps: number | null;
  change_24h_bps: number | null;
  change_7d_bps: number | null;
  status: "ok" | "watch" | "alert" | "na";
  venues: VenueRow[];
  latest_ts: string | null;
};

export async function fetchTicker(
  ticker: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<TickerDetail> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RADAR_API_BASE;
  if (!base) throw new Error("API base URL is not configured");
  const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/ticker?ticker=${encodeURIComponent(ticker)}`, {
    cache: "no-store",
    signal,
  });
  if (res.status === 404) throw new Error("NOT_FOUND");
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`API HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return (await res.json()) as TickerDetail;
}

export type HistoryPoint = { ts: string; dev_bps: number | null };

export async function fetchHistory(
  ticker: string,
  window: "1h" | "24h" | "7d",
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<HistoryPoint[]> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RADAR_API_BASE;
  if (!base) throw new Error("API base URL is not configured");
  const res = await fetch(
    `${base}/api/history?ticker=${encodeURIComponent(ticker)}&window=${window}`,
    { cache: "no-store", signal },
  );
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`API HTTP ${res.status}`);
  const d = (await res.json()) as { points: HistoryPoint[] };
  return d.points;
}

/* Coverage (chain x issuer x DEX), 2026-11-12 — GET /api/coverage.
   `live` rows are derived from the pools actually polled in Turso;
   `pending` rows are the dated 12 Nov 2026 verification verdicts,
   reference data, never live claims. `reason_key` is translated by
   the frontend's own i18n (cov_reason_*). */
export type CoverageLiveGroup = {
  chain: string;
  chain_display: string;
  issuer: string;
  issuer_display: string;
  dexes: string[];
  tickers: string[];
  pools: number;
  status: "live";
};

export type CoveragePendingRow = {
  chain: string;
  chain_display: string;
  issuer: string;
  issuer_display: string;
  platforms: string;
  tokens: string;
  verdict: "verified" | "failed" | "not_active" | "monitor";
  reason_key: string;
};

export type CoverageResponse = {
  live: CoverageLiveGroup[];
  pending: CoveragePendingRow[];
  verdict_date: string;
  live_tickers: number;
  live_pools: number;
  only_solana_xstocks: boolean;
  generated_at: string | null;
};

export async function fetchCoverage(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<CoverageResponse> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RADAR_API_BASE;
  if (!base) throw new Error("API base URL is not configured");
  const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/coverage`, { cache: "no-store", signal });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`API HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return (await res.json()) as CoverageResponse;
}

export type WaitlistPayload = {
  contact: string;
  position: string;
  venues: string;
  price?: string;
  consent: true;
  /** Honeypot: always "" from the real form; a filled value is a bot. */
  website?: string;
};

export type WaitlistResult = { ok: true; already: boolean };

export async function postWaitlist(p: WaitlistPayload): Promise<WaitlistResult> {
  const base = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RADAR_API_BASE;
  if (!base) throw new Error("API base URL is not configured");
  const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/waitlist`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(p),
  });
  const d = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
    ok?: boolean;
    already?: boolean;
    error?: string;
  };
  if (!res.ok || !d.ok) throw new Error(d.error || `API HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return { ok: true, already: !!d.already };
}


===== KODE: web/src/lib/risk.ts =====
/* Weekend risk score v1 — verbatim port of dashboard/risk.py.
 * Formula: deviation |dev_pct|*10 capped 40; depth 0 at >=$2.5M, 25 at
 * <=$250k, linear between; redemption closed=20, gated=10, open=0,
 * unknown/None excluded (not zeroed); PoR 15*age/72 capped 15, None
 * excluded. Total normalized to 0-100 over available maxima.
 * ALWAYS labeled "experimental" until calibrated on >= 8 weekends. */
export type RiskScore = {
  components: {
    deviation: number | null;
    depth: number | null;
    redemption: number | null;
    por: number | null;
  };
  total: number;
  max_possible: number;
  score_100: number | null;
  components_present: number;
  partial: boolean;
};

const DEPTH_FLOOR = 250_000;
const DEPTH_FULL = 2_500_000;

function clamp(x: number, lo: number, hi: number): number {
  return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, x));
}

/** Python round(x, 1) semantics (banker's rounding) — JS Math.round
 *  is half-up and would differ by 0.1 on exact .05 boundaries. */
function pyRound1(x: number): number {
  const m = x * 10;
  const f = Math.floor(m);
  const diff = m - f;
  if (diff > 0.5) return (f + 1) / 10;
  if (diff < 0.5) return f / 10;
  return (f % 2 === 0 ? f : f + 1) / 10;
}

export function weekendRiskScore(
  devPct: number | null,
  depthUsd: number | null,
  redemptionStatus: string | null,
  porAgeHours: number | null,
): RiskScore {
  const deviation = devPct == null ? null : pyRound1(clamp(Math.abs(devPct) * 10, 0, 40));
  let depth: number | null = null;
  if (depthUsd != null) {
    if (depthUsd <= DEPTH_FLOOR) depth = 25;
    else if (depthUsd >= DEPTH_FULL) depth = 0;
    else depth = pyRound1(25 * (1 - (depthUsd - DEPTH_FLOOR) / (DEPTH_FULL - DEPTH_FLOOR)));
  }
  const redMap: Record<string, number> = { closed: 20, gated: 10, open: 0 };
  const redemption =
    redemptionStatus == null ? null : (redMap[redemptionStatus.trim().toLowerCase()] ?? null);
  const por =
    porAgeHours == null
      ? null
      : porAgeHours >= 72
        ? 15
        : pyRound1((15 * Math.max(porAgeHours, 0)) / 72);
  const comps = { deviation, depth, redemption, por };
  const maxima = { deviation: 40, depth: 25, redemption: 20, por: 15 };
  const avail = (Object.keys(comps) as (keyof typeof comps)[]).filter((k) => comps[k] != null);
  const total = Math.round(avail.reduce((s, k) => s + (comps[k] ?? 0), 0) * 10) / 10;
  const maxPossible = avail.reduce((s, k) => s + maxima[k], 0);
  return {
    components: comps,
    total,
    max_possible: maxPossible,
    score_100: maxPossible ? Math.round((total / maxPossible) * 1000) / 10 : null,
    components_present: avail.length,
    partial: avail.length < 4,
  };
}

/** Exit Score display band (design §2.3, display only — the score
 *  itself is computed by the poller and stored, never computed here):
 *  0–39 low, 40–69 medium, 70–100 high. Null (or out-of-range) maps
 *  to null: a missing score is never shown as 0, and an impossible
 *  score is never banded. Mirrors dashboard/risk.py exit_score_band. */
export function exitScoreBand(score: number | null): "low" | "medium" | "high" | null {
  if (score == null || !Number.isFinite(score)) return null;
  if (score < 0 || score > 100) return null;
  if (score < 40) return "low";
  if (score < 70) return "medium";
  return "high";
}
